```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::hooks::wire::Payload;

    fn payload(rel: &str) -> Payload {
        let p = format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"));
        Payload::from_value(&serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap()).unwrap()
    }
    fn screen(name: &str) -> String {
        std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
    }
    fn k() -> CorrelationKey { CorrelationKey("k".into()) }

    #[test]
    fn claude_permission_is_a_held_hook_request() {
        let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"));
        assert_eq!((o.kind, o.answer_path, o.ready, o.reusable_key), (RequestKind::Permission, AnswerPath::Hook, true, true));
        assert_eq!(o.title, "Bash");
        assert_eq!(o.detail, "touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt");
        let ids: Vec<_> = o.options.iter().map(|x| (x.id.as_str(), x.effect)).collect();
        assert_eq!(ids, vec![("allow", Effect::Allow), ("deny", Effect::Deny)], "no addRules suggestion, no allow_always");
    }

    #[test]
    fn allow_always_shows_the_exact_rule() {
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
        p.permission_suggestions = vec![serde_json::json!({"type": "addRules",
            "rules": [{"toolName": "Bash", "ruleContent": "touch /tmp/acp0/*"}], "behavior": "allow", "destination": "session"})];
        let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &p);
        let always = o.options.iter().find(|x| x.effect == Effect::AllowAlways).unwrap();
        assert!(always.description.as_deref().unwrap().contains("Bash(touch /tmp/acp0/*)"));
        assert!(always.description.as_deref().unwrap().contains(".claude/settings.local.json"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn claude_keys_after_the_hold_drop_allow_always_and_use_the_no_option() {
        let dialog = crate::herdr::screen::find_dialog(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).unwrap();
        let keys = claude_permission_keys(&dialog);
        assert_eq!(keys.get("allow").map(String::as_str), Some("1"));
        assert_eq!(keys.get("deny").map(String::as_str), Some("4"), "option 4 is \"No\" in the fixture");
        assert_eq!(keys.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(claude_keys_options().len(), 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_question_is_answerable_only_when_bound() {
        let p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
        let bound = claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((bound.kind, bound.answer_path, bound.ready), (RequestKind::Question, AnswerPath::Keys, false));
        assert_eq!(bound.options.iter().map(|o| o.label.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["Red", "Blue"]);
        assert_eq!(bound.options[0].id, "o1");
        assert_eq!(bound.questions.as_ref().unwrap()[0].text, "Pick a color");
        assert!(!bound.free_text, "Type something and Chat about this are never options");
        let unbound = claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(unbound.answer_path, AnswerPath::None);
        assert_eq!(question_keys(2), [("o1".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("o2".to_string(), "2".to_string())].into());
    }

    #[test]
    fn multi_select_or_many_questions_stay_read_only() {
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
        p.tool_input["questions"][0]["multiSelect"] = true.into();
        assert_eq!(claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::None);
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
        let q = p.tool_input["questions"][0].clone();
        p.tool_input["questions"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push(q);
        assert_eq!(claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn codex_permission_uses_keys_and_shows_the_command_then_the_reason() {
        let o = codex_permission("a", k(), None, &payload("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), false);
        assert_eq!((o.answer_path, o.ready, o.reusable_key), (AnswerPath::Keys, false, true));
        assert!(o.detail.starts_with("touch ~/acp0_test_1.txt\n"));
        assert!(o.detail.contains("outside the writable workspace"));
        assert_eq!(o.options.iter().map(|x| x.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["allow", "allow_always", "deny"]);
        let collapsed = codex_permission("a", k(), None, &payload("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), true);
        assert!(collapsed.detail.ends_with("Enlarge the pane to answer here."));
    }

    #[test]
    fn hostile_text_is_cleaned_and_capped() {
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
        p.tool_name = Some("Ba\u{1b}[31msh\u{202E}".into());
        p.tool_input["command"] = format!("rm -rf /\u{7}{}", "x".repeat(5000)).into();
        let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &p);
        assert_eq!(o.title, "Ba[31msh");
        assert!(o.detail.chars().count() <= DETAIL_CHARS && !o.detail.contains('\u{7}'));
    }

    #[test]
    fn logged_payloads_are_redacted() {
        let s = payload_for_log(br#"{"tool_input":{"command":"GITHUB_TOKEN=ghp_secret make"}}"#);
        assert!(!s.contains("ghp_secret"), "{s}");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `wire.rs`**

```rust
//! Hook payloads, names and replies (spec §6.1 to §6.3). Payloads are read
//! field by field: a field of the wrong type is absent, never fatal, so a
//! future Claude or Codex version fails open instead of breaking acd.
use acd_core::CorrelationKey;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

pub const MAX_ID_BYTES: usize = 128;
pub const MAX_HINT_BYTES: usize = 1024;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum HookAgent {
    Claude,
    Codex,
}

impl HookAgent {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Option<HookAgent> {
        match s {
            "claude" => Some(HookAgent::Claude),
            "codex" => Some(HookAgent::Codex),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum HookName {
    SessionStart,
    UserPromptSubmit,
    PermissionRequest,
    PreToolUse,
    PostToolUse,
    Stop,
    SessionEnd,
    /// Notification and anything newer: logged and ignored.
    Other,
}

impl HookName {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> HookName {
        match s {
            "SessionStart" => HookName::SessionStart,
            "UserPromptSubmit" => HookName::UserPromptSubmit,
            "PermissionRequest" => HookName::PermissionRequest,
            "PreToolUse" => HookName::PreToolUse,
            "PostToolUse" => HookName::PostToolUse,
            "Stop" => HookName::Stop,
            "SessionEnd" => HookName::SessionEnd,
            _ => HookName::Other,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct Payload {
    pub session_id: String,
    pub prompt_id: Option<String>,
    pub turn_id: Option<String>,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
    pub tool_name: Option<String>,
    pub tool_input: Value,
    pub tool_response: Value,
    pub permission_suggestions: Vec<Value>,
    pub last_assistant_message: Option<String>,
    /// Present on subagent payloads, which are always dropped (spec §6.2).
    pub agent_id: Option<String>,
}

/// 1 to 128 bytes, no control characters.
pub fn valid_id(s: &str) -> bool {
    !s.is_empty() && s.len() <= MAX_ID_BYTES && !s.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

impl Payload {
    pub fn from_value(v: &Value) -> Option<Payload> {
        let o = v.as_object()?;
        let s = |k: &str| o.get(k).and_then(Value::as_str).map(str::to_owned);
        let id = |k: &str| s(k).filter(|x| valid_id(x));
        Some(Payload {
            session_id: id("session_id")?,
            prompt_id: id("prompt_id"),
            turn_id: id("turn_id"),
            cwd: s("cwd"),
            tool_name: s("tool_name"),
            tool_input: o.get("tool_input").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
            tool_response: o.get("tool_response").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
            permission_suggestions: o
                .get("permission_suggestions")
                .and_then(Value::as_array)
                .cloned()
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            last_assistant_message: s("last_assistant_message"),
            agent_id: s("agent_id"),
        })
    }

    pub fn is_subagent(&self) -> bool {
        self.agent_id.as_deref().is_some_and(|a| !a.is_empty())
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Hints {
    pub herdr_pane: Option<String>,
    pub herdr_socket: Option<String>,
    pub entrypoint: Option<String>,
}

/// A header hint, or `None` when empty, too long or holding control chars.
pub fn hint(v: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
    v.filter(|s| !s.is_empty() && s.len() <= MAX_HINT_BYTES && !s.chars().any(char::is_control))
        .map(str::to_owned)
}

impl Hints {
    pub fn from_headers(h: &axum::http::HeaderMap) -> Hints {
        let get = |name: &str| hint(h.get(name).and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()));
        Hints {
            herdr_pane: get("x-herdr-pane"),
            herdr_socket: get("x-herdr-socket"),
            entrypoint: get("x-claude-entrypoint"),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct HookEvent {
    pub agent: HookAgent,
    pub name: HookName,
    pub payload: Payload,
    pub hints: Hints,
    pub at: tokio::time::Instant,
}

/// Compact JSON with object keys sorted at every level.
pub fn canonical_json(v: &Value) -> String {
    fn write(v: &Value, out: &mut String) {
        match v {
            Value::Object(m) => {
                let mut keys: Vec<&String> = m.keys().collect();
                keys.sort();
                out.push('{');
                for (i, k) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
                    if i > 0 {
                        out.push(',');
                    }
                    out.push_str(&Value::String((*k).clone()).to_string());
                    out.push(':');
                    write(&m[*k], out);
                }
                out.push('}');
            }
            Value::Array(a) => {
                out.push('[');
                for (i, x) in a.iter().enumerate() {
                    if i > 0 {
                        out.push(',');
                    }
                    write(x, out);
                }
                out.push(']');
            }
            other => out.push_str(&other.to_string()),
        }
    }
    let mut s = String::new();
    write(v, &mut s);
    s
}

/// Lowercase hex `sha256(tool_name ‖ 0x00 ‖ canonical_json(tool_input))`.
pub fn tool_digest(tool_name: &str, input: &Value) -> String {
    let mut h = Sha256::new();
    h.update(tool_name.as_bytes());
    h.update([0u8]);
    h.update(canonical_json(input).as_bytes());
    h.finalize().iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
}

pub fn command_of(input: &Value) -> Option<&str> {
    input.get("command").and_then(Value::as_str)
}

/// `claude:<session>:<prompt_id>:<digest>` (D2).
pub fn claude_key(p: &Payload) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
    Some(CorrelationKey(format!(
        "claude:{}:{}:{}",
        p.session_id,
        p.prompt_id.as_deref()?,
        tool_digest(p.tool_name.as_deref()?, &p.tool_input)
    )))
}

/// `codex:<session>:<turn_id>:<digest>` (spec §5.1).
pub fn codex_key(p: &Payload) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
    Some(CorrelationKey(format!(
        "codex:{}:{}:{}",
        p.session_id,
        p.turn_id.as_deref()?,
        tool_digest(p.tool_name.as_deref()?, &p.tool_input)
    )))
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct AlwaysRule {
    pub tool_name: String,
    pub rule_content: String,
}

/// The first `addRules` suggestion's first rule (spec §6.2 step 1).
pub fn always_rule(suggestions: &[Value]) -> Option<AlwaysRule> {
    suggestions.iter().find_map(|s| {
        if s.get("type")?.as_str()? != "addRules" {
            return None;
        }
        let r = s.get("rules")?.as_array()?.first()?;
        Some(AlwaysRule {
            tool_name: r.get("toolName")?.as_str()?.to_owned(),
            rule_content: r.get("ruleContent")?.as_str()?.to_owned(),
        })
    })
}

fn envelope(decision: Value) -> String {
    json!({"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": "PermissionRequest", "decision": decision}}).to_string()
}

pub fn reply_allow() -> String {
    envelope(json!({"behavior": "allow"}))
}

pub fn reply_deny() -> String {
    envelope(json!({"behavior": "deny", "message": "Denied from agent inbox"}))
}

/// Allow, plus the suggestion's rule persisted to `localSettings`, the only
/// destination P0 and gate 2e saw persist.
pub fn reply_allow_always(r: &AlwaysRule) -> String {
    envelope(json!({
        "behavior": "allow",
        "updatedPermissions": [{
            "type": "addRules",
            "rules": [{"toolName": r.tool_name, "ruleContent": r.rule_content}],
            "behavior": "allow",
            "destination": "localSettings"
        }]
    }))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `mapping.rs`**

```rust
//! Hook payloads to registry observations (spec §6.2, §6.3).
use std::collections::HashMap;

use acd_core::{CorrelationKey, RequestObs};
use acd_protocol::{AnswerPath, Effect, Question, RequestKind, RequestOption};
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::{HookAgent, HookName, Payload, always_rule, canonical_json, command_of};
use crate::herdr::screen::Dialog;
use crate::text::{TITLE_CHARS, cap_chars, clean_display, clean_screen, redact_log};

pub const DETAIL_CHARS: usize = 4000;
const DESCRIPTION_CHARS: usize = 400;
pub const CODEX_KEYS: [(&str, &str); 3] = [("allow", "1"), ("allow_always", "2"), ("deny", "esc")];

fn opt(id: &str, label: &str, effect: Effect, description: Option<String>) -> RequestOption {
    RequestOption { id: id.into(), label: label.into(), description, effect }
}

fn detail(raw: &str) -> String {
    cap_chars(clean_screen(raw).trim(), DETAIL_CHARS)
}

/// The command, the file path, or compact JSON (spec §6.2 step 1).
pub fn detail_of(input: &Value) -> String {
    if let Some(c) = command_of(input) {
        return detail(c);
    }
    if let Some(f) = input.get("file_path").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        return detail(f);
    }
    detail(&canonical_json(input))
}

pub fn claude_permission(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
) -> RequestObs {
    let mut options = vec![opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None), opt("deny", "No", Effect::Deny, None)];
    if let Some(rule) = always_rule(&p.permission_suggestions) {
        let shown = clean_display(&format!("{}({})", rule.tool_name, rule.rule_content), DESCRIPTION_CHARS);
        options.insert(
            1,
            opt(
                "allow_always",
                "Yes, always",
                Effect::AllowAlways,
                Some(format!("Adds the rule {shown} to .claude/settings.local.json in this project")),
            ),
        );
    }
    RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Permission,
        answer_path: AnswerPath::Hook,
        title: clean_display(p.tool_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("Permission"), TITLE_CHARS),
        detail: detail_of(&p.tool_input),
        options,
        questions: None,
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: true,
        reusable_key: true,
    }
}

/// Options of a Claude permission request on the keys path (D9).
pub fn claude_keys_options() -> Vec<RequestOption> {
    vec![opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None), opt("deny", "No", Effect::Deny, None)]
}

/// `allow` is option 1; `deny` is the option labelled `No`, else `esc`.
pub fn claude_permission_keys(dialog: &Dialog) -> HashMap<String, String> {
    let no = dialog
        .options
        .iter()
        .find(|o| o.label.trim() == "No")
        .map(|o| o.number.to_string())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "esc".into());
    [("allow".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("deny".to_string(), no)].into()
}

pub fn question_keys(n: usize) -> HashMap<String, String> {
    (1..=n.min(9)).map(|i| (format!("o{i}"), i.to_string())).collect()
}

/// `bound`: the session is bound to a herdr pane. Only a single,
/// single-select question with 1 to 9 options on a bound session is
/// answerable (spec §6.2 Questions); its path is `keys`, not ready until
/// the dialog is captured.
pub fn claude_question(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
    bound: bool,
) -> Option<RequestObs> {
    let raw = p.tool_input.get("questions")?.as_array()?;
    let questions: Vec<Question> = raw
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter_map(|(qi, q)| {
            let text = q.get("question")?.as_str()?;
            let options = q
                .get("options")?
                .as_array()?
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .filter_map(|(i, o)| {
                    Some(opt(
                        &format!("o{}", i + 1),
                        &clean_display(o.get("label")?.as_str()?, TITLE_CHARS),
                        Effect::Choose,
                        o.get("description").and_then(Value::as_str).map(|d| clean_display(d, DESCRIPTION_CHARS)),
                    ))
                })
                .collect();
            Some(Question {
                id: format!("q{}", qi + 1),
                header: q.get("header").and_then(Value::as_str).map(|h| clean_display(h, TITLE_CHARS)),
                text: detail(text),
                multi_select: q.get("multiSelect").and_then(Value::as_bool).unwrap_or(false),
                options,
            })
        })
        .collect();
    let first = questions.first()?.clone();
    let answerable = bound && questions.len() == 1 && !first.multi_select && (1..=9).contains(&first.options.len());
    Some(RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Question,
        answer_path: if answerable { AnswerPath::Keys } else { AnswerPath::None },
        title: first.header.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "Question".into()),
        detail: first.text.clone(),
        options: first.options.clone(),
        questions: Some(questions),
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: false,
        reusable_key: true,
    })
}

/// spec §6.3 `PermissionRequest`. `collapsed`: the pane is too short and
/// the dialog hides the command, so it can never be matched.
pub fn codex_permission(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
    collapsed: bool,
) -> RequestObs {
    let mut text = command_of(&p.tool_input).unwrap_or_default().to_owned();
    if let Some(d) = p.tool_input.get("description").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        text.push('\n');
        text.push_str(d);
    }
    let mut detail = detail(&text);
    if collapsed {
        detail.push_str("\n\nEnlarge the pane to answer here.");
    }
    RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Permission,
        answer_path: AnswerPath::Keys,
        title: clean_display(p.tool_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("Permission"), TITLE_CHARS),
        detail,
        options: vec![
            opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None),
            opt("allow_always", "Yes, don't ask again", Effect::AllowAlways, None),
            opt("deny", "No, and tell Codex what to do differently", Effect::Deny, None),
        ],
        questions: None,
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: false,
        reusable_key: true,
    }
}

pub fn payload_for_log(raw: &[u8]) -> String {
    redact_log(&String::from_utf8_lossy(raw))
}

/// Payloads only at debug, redacted and truncated (spec §7.1 Logs).
pub fn log_payload(agent: HookAgent, name: &HookName, raw: &[u8]) {
    if tracing::enabled!(tracing::Level::DEBUG) {
        tracing::debug!(?agent, ?name, payload = %payload_for_log(raw), "hook payload");
    }
}
```

### Task 7: Hook endpoint: auth, limits, holds, close detection

PENDING GATE 2: an empty header value counts as absent, because an
unlisted variable in a Claude header interpolates to `""` (draft 2a).
The close-detection test assumes Claude closes the held connection on a
terminal deny or Esc (draft 2b); the endpoint only reports the close,
Task 11c decides what it means.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test client**

`hooks/testing.rs` (behind `#[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]` in

```rust
//! A raw HTTP/1.1 client for hook tests: no client library, and it can
//! close a connection mid-hold the way Claude does.
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

pub async fn send(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> TcpStream {
    let mut s = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).await.unwrap();
    let mut req = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    if !token.is_empty() {
        req.push_str(&format!("X-Acd-Token: {token}\r\n"));
    }
    for (k, v) in headers {
        req.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\r\n"));
    }
    req.push_str("\r\n");
    req.push_str(body);
    s.write_all(req.as_bytes()).await.unwrap();
    s
}

pub async fn read_response(mut s: TcpStream) -> (u16, String) {
    let mut buf = Vec::new();
    // A reset after the reply (unread request bytes) keeps what was read.
    let _ = s.read_to_end(&mut buf).await;
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf).into_owned();
    let status = text.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|c| c.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    let body = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n").map(|(_, b)| b.to_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    (status, body)
}

pub async fn post(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> (u16, String) {
    read_response(send(port, path, token, headers, body).await).await
}
```

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::*;
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::{post, read_response, send};
use acd_adapters::hooks::wire::HookName;
use tokio::sync::mpsc;
use tokio::time::{Instant, timeout};

const TOKEN: &str = "tok-current-0123456789";

async fn serve(opts: EndpointOptions) -> (u16, Arc<HookAuth>, mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Inbound>) {
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
    let auth = Arc::new(HookAuth::new(TOKEN.into()));
    let app = router(auth.clone(), tx, opts);
    tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap() });
    (port, auth, rx)
}

fn fx(rel: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

async fn next(rx: &mut mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Inbound>) -> Inbound {
    timeout(Duration::from_secs(2), rx.recv()).await.expect("inbound in time").unwrap()
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn rejects_bad_tokens_methods_paths_and_agents() {
    let (port, _auth, _rx) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    let body = fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json");
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", "", &[], &body).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", "wrong", &[], &body).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/aider/UserPromptSubmit", TOKEN, &[], &body).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/elsewhere", TOKEN, &[], &body).await.0, 401);
    let mut s = tokio::net::TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).await.unwrap();
    tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt::write_all(&mut s,
        format!("GET /hook/claude/Stop HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\nX-Acd-Token: {TOKEN}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n").as_bytes()).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(read_response(s).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit?marker=acd-managed:v1", TOKEN, &[], &body).await.0, 200, "the query is ignored");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn previous_token_is_accepted_until_cleared() {
    let (port, auth, mut rx) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    auth.set("tok-new-0123456789".into(), Some(TOKEN.into()));
    let body = fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json");
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", TOKEN, &[], &body).await.0, 200);
    assert!(matches!(next(&mut rx).await, Inbound::Event { token: TokenUse::Previous, .. }));
    auth.set("tok-new-0123456789".into(), None);
    let before = Instant::now();
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", TOKEN, &[("X-Herdr-Pane", "w1:p1")], &body).await.0, 401);
    assert_eq!(auth.rejected_since(before), 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn lifecycle_hooks_reply_at_once_without_a_consumer() {
    let (port, _auth, _rx) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await; // rx never read
    let t = Instant::now();
    let (status, body) = post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-Stop.json")).await;
    assert_eq!((status, body.as_str()), (200, ""));
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(200));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_held_permission_returns_the_adapter_reply() {
    let (port, _auth, mut rx) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    let s = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN,
        &[("X-Herdr-Pane", "w1:p2"), ("X-Herdr-Socket", ""), ("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")],
        &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")).await;
    let Inbound::Held { event, reply, .. } = next(&mut rx).await else { panic!("not held") };
    assert_eq!(event.name, HookName::PermissionRequest);
    assert_eq!(event.hints.herdr_pane.as_deref(), Some("w1:p2"));
    assert_eq!(event.hints.herdr_socket, None, "an empty interpolation is absent");
    reply.send(acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_allow()).unwrap();
    let (status, body) = read_response(s).await;
    assert_eq!(status, 200);
    assert_eq!(body, acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_allow());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_released_hold_replies_empty() {
    let (port, _auth, mut rx) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    let s = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")).await;
    let Inbound::Held { reply, .. } = next(&mut rx).await else { panic!() };
    drop(reply);
    assert_eq!(read_response(s).await, (200, String::new()));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_client_close_is_seen_as_hold_closed() {
    let (port, _auth, mut rx) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    let s = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")).await;
    let Inbound::Held { hold, reply, .. } = next(&mut rx).await else { panic!() };
    drop(s); // Claude closes the connection on a terminal deny or Esc (gate 2b)
    let t = Instant::now();
    match next(&mut rx).await {
        Inbound::HoldClosed { hold: h } => assert_eq!(h, hold),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(1), "close seen within 1 s");
    assert!(reply.is_closed(), "a reply after the close can never be delivered");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn hold_expiry_replies_empty_and_reports_it() {
    let (port, _auth, mut rx) = serve(EndpointOptions { permission_hold: Duration::from_millis(200), ..Default::default() }).await;
    let s = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &[], &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")).await;
    let Inbound::Held { hold, reply: _keep, .. } = next(&mut rx).await else { panic!() };
    assert_eq!(read_response(s).await, (200, String::new()));
    assert!(matches!(next(&mut rx).await, Inbound::HoldExpired { hold: h } if h == hold));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn only_claude_tool_permissions_are_held() {
    let (port, _auth, mut rx) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    for (path, rel) in [
        ("/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", "claude/payload-PermissionRequest-AskUserQuestion.json"),
        ("/hook/codex/PermissionRequest", "codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"),
    ] {
        assert_eq!(post(port, path, TOKEN, &[], &fx(rel)).await, (200, String::new()), "{path}");
        assert!(matches!(next(&mut rx).await, Inbound::Event { .. }));
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn holds_beyond_the_cap_fail_open() {
    let (port, _auth, mut rx) = serve(EndpointOptions { max_holds: 1, ..Default::default() }).await;
    let body = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    let _first = send(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &[], &body).await;
    let Inbound::Held { reply: _keep, .. } = next(&mut rx).await else { panic!() };
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &[], &body).await, (200, String::new()));
    assert!(matches!(next(&mut rx).await, Inbound::Event { .. }));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn oversized_and_non_object_bodies_fail_open() {
    let (port, _auth, mut rx) = serve(EndpointOptions::default()).await;
    let big = format!("{{\"session_id\":\"s\",\"x\":\"{}\"}}", "a".repeat(1 << 20));
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], &big).await.0, 413);
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], "[1,2]").await, (200, String::new()));
    assert_eq!(post(port, "/hook/claude/Stop", TOKEN, &[], "{\"cwd\":\"/\"}").await, (200, String::new()));
    assert!(timeout(Duration::from_millis(200), rx.recv()).await.is_err(), "nothing reaches the adapter");
}
```

`hooks/mod.rs` gains `pub mod endpoint;` and

`hooks/endpoint.rs`:

```rust
//! The hook endpoint (spec §6.1): `POST /hook/{agent}/{event}` on
//! 127.0.0.1. Lifecycle hooks get their reply before any processing. A
//! Claude `PermissionRequest` for a tool is parked as a hold until the
//! adapter replies, releases it, or `permission_hold` passes.
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, RwLock};
use std::time::Duration;

use axum::Router;
use axum::extract::{Path, Request, State};
use axum::http::{Method, StatusCode, header};
use axum::response::{IntoResponse, Response};
use axum::routing::any;
use subtle::ConstantTimeEq;
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};
use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::mapping::log_payload;
use super::wire::{HookAgent, HookEvent, HookName, Hints, Payload};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TokenUse {
    Current,
    Previous,
}

struct Tokens {
    current: String,
    previous: Option<String>,
}

pub struct HookAuth {
    tokens: RwLock<Tokens>,
    /// Remote hint -> last rejection, for rate-limited logs and status.
    rejected: Mutex<HashMap<String, Instant>>,
}

impl HookAuth {
    pub fn new(current: String) -> HookAuth {
        HookAuth {
            tokens: RwLock::new(Tokens { current, previous: None }),
            rejected: Mutex::default(),
        }
    }

    pub fn set(&self, current: String, previous: Option<String>) {
        *self.tokens.write().expect("hook auth") = Tokens { current, previous };
    }

    /// Constant-time comparison. An empty stored token never matches.
    pub fn check(&self, presented: &str) -> Option<TokenUse> {
        let t = self.tokens.read().expect("hook auth");
        let eq = |stored: &str| !stored.is_empty() && bool::from(stored.as_bytes().ct_eq(presented.as_bytes()));
        if eq(&t.current) {
            Some(TokenUse::Current)
        } else if t.previous.as_deref().is_some_and(eq) {
            Some(TokenUse::Previous)
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    /// Logs at most once a minute per hint; never logs the token.
    pub fn note_rejected(&self, hint: &str) {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut r = self.rejected.lock().expect("hook auth");
        let quiet = r.get(hint).is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(*t) < Duration::from_secs(60));
        r.insert(hint.to_owned(), now);
        if r.len() > 1024 {
            r.retain(|_, t| now.duration_since(*t) < Duration::from_secs(3600));
        }
        if !quiet {
            tracing::warn!(remote = %hint, "hook request with a missing or retired token (restart or /clear that Claude session)");
        }
    }

    pub fn rejected_since(&self, since: Instant) -> usize {
        self.rejected.lock().expect("hook auth").values().filter(|t| **t >= since).count()
    }
}

pub type HoldId = u64;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum Inbound {
    Event { event: HookEvent, token: TokenUse },
    Held { event: HookEvent, hold: HoldId, reply: oneshot::Sender<String>, token: TokenUse },
    /// The client closed the connection before acd replied.
    HoldClosed { hold: HoldId },
    /// `permission_hold` passed; the endpoint already replied empty.
    HoldExpired { hold: HoldId },
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct EndpointOptions {
    pub permission_hold: Duration,
    pub max_holds: usize,
    pub body_timeout: Duration,
    pub max_body: usize,
}

impl Default for EndpointOptions {
    fn default() -> Self {
        EndpointOptions {
            permission_hold: Duration::from_secs(3600),
            max_holds: 64,
            body_timeout: Duration::from_secs(5),
            max_body: 1 << 20,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Ctx {
    auth: Arc<HookAuth>,
    tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>,
    opts: EndpointOptions,
    holds: Arc<AtomicUsize>,
    next: Arc<AtomicU64>,
}

pub fn router(auth: Arc<HookAuth>, tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>, opts: EndpointOptions) -> Router {
    let ctx = Ctx { auth, tx, opts, holds: Arc::default(), next: Arc::default() };
    Router::new()
        .route("/hook/{agent}/{event}", any(handle))
        .fallback(|| async { StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED })
        .with_state(ctx)
}

fn empty() -> Response {
    StatusCode::OK.into_response()
}

async fn handle(State(ctx): State<Ctx>, Path((agent, event)): Path<(String, String)>, req: Request) -> Response {
    if req.method() != Method::POST {
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    }
    let Some(agent) = HookAgent::parse(&agent) else {
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    };
    let hints = Hints::from_headers(req.headers());
    let presented = req.headers().get("x-acd-token").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()).unwrap_or("");
    let Some(token) = ctx.auth.check(presented) else {
        ctx.auth.note_rejected(hints.herdr_pane.as_deref().unwrap_or("unknown pane"));
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    };
    let body = match tokio::time::timeout(ctx.opts.body_timeout, axum::body::to_bytes(req.into_body(), ctx.opts.max_body)).await {
        Ok(Ok(b)) => b,
        Ok(Err(_)) => return StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE.into_response(),
        Err(_) => return StatusCode::REQUEST_TIMEOUT.into_response(),
    };
    let name = HookName::parse(&event);
    log_payload(agent, &name, &body);
    let Some(payload) = serde_json::from_slice(&body).ok().and_then(|v| Payload::from_value(&v)) else {
        return empty();
    };
    let holdable = agent == HookAgent::Claude
        && name == HookName::PermissionRequest
        && payload.tool_name.as_deref() != Some("AskUserQuestion");
    let event = HookEvent { agent, name, payload, hints, at: Instant::now() };
    if !holdable {
        let _ = ctx.tx.send(Inbound::Event { event, token });
        return empty();
    }
    if ctx.holds.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) >= ctx.opts.max_holds {
        ctx.holds.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        tracing::warn!("too many held permission prompts; this one is answered in the terminal");
        let _ = ctx.tx.send(Inbound::Event { event, token });
        return empty();
    }
    hold(ctx, event, token).await
}

/// Reports a close when dropped before a reply was taken. hyper drops the
/// handler future when the client closes the connection, so a guard drop
/// without `done` is the "answered in the terminal or interrupted" signal.
struct HoldGuard {
    tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>,
    hold: HoldId,
    holds: Arc<AtomicUsize>,
    done: bool,
}

impl Drop for HoldGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.holds.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        if !self.done {
            let _ = self.tx.send(Inbound::HoldClosed { hold: self.hold });
        }
    }
}

async fn hold(ctx: Ctx, event: HookEvent, token: TokenUse) -> Response {
    let id = ctx.next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    let (reply_tx, reply_rx) = oneshot::channel::<String>();
    let mut guard = HoldGuard { tx: ctx.tx.clone(), hold: id, holds: ctx.holds.clone(), done: false };
    if ctx.tx.send(Inbound::Held { event, hold: id, reply: reply_tx, token }).is_err() {
        guard.done = true;
        return empty();
    }
    let out = tokio::time::timeout(ctx.opts.permission_hold, reply_rx).await;
    guard.done = true;
    match out {
        Ok(Ok(body)) if !body.is_empty() => {
            ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json")], body).into_response()
        }
        Ok(_) => empty(),
        Err(_) => {
            let _ = ctx.tx.send(Inbound::HoldExpired { hold: id });
            empty()
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```rust
//! `~/.claude/sessions/<pid>.json` discovery (spec §6.2, gate 5). Read
//! only `<digits>.json`; never open the `.key` files. Best effort: an
//! entry that cannot be read or verified is skipped and logged once.
use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

use acd_protocol::AgentState;
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::valid_id;

const MAX_ENTRY_BYTES: u64 = 64 * 1024;
/// Owner-only writes: a group- or world-writable entry is not trusted.
const MAX_ENTRY_MODE: u32 = 0o755;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RegistryEntry {
    pub pid: i32,
    pub session_id: String,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
    pub started_at_ms: Option<i64>,
    pub proc_start: Option<String>,
    pub entrypoint: Option<String>,
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub status: Option<String>,
    pub waiting_for: Option<String>,
}

impl RegistryEntry {
    pub fn from_value(v: &Value) -> Option<RegistryEntry> {
        let o = v.as_object()?;
        let s = |k: &str| o.get(k).and_then(Value::as_str).map(str::to_owned);
        let pid = o.get("pid")?.as_i64().filter(|p| *p > 0 && *p <= i32::MAX as i64)? as i32;
        Some(RegistryEntry {
            pid,
            session_id: s("sessionId").filter(|x| valid_id(x))?,
            cwd: s("cwd"),
            started_at_ms: o.get("startedAt").and_then(Value::as_i64),
            proc_start: s("procStart"),
            entrypoint: s("entrypoint"),
            name: s("name"),
            status: s("status"),
            waiting_for: s("waitingFor"),
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ProcVerdict {
    Live,
    Gone,
    Unverifiable,
}

pub trait ProcCheck: Send + Sync {
    fn check(&self, pid: i32, proc_start: &str) -> ProcVerdict;
}

/// `ps -o lstart=` in UTC (gate 5): `Fri Oct  9 07:29:36 2026`.
pub fn parse_lstart_utc(s: &str) -> Option<i64> {
    let t: Vec<&str> = s.split_whitespace().collect();
    let [_, mon, day, hms, year] = t.as_slice() else { return None };
    const MONTHS: [&str; 12] = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
    let month = MONTHS.iter().position(|m| m == mon)? as u32 + 1;
    let mut hms = hms.split(':').map(|x| x.parse::<u32>().ok());
    let (h, m, sec) = (hms.next()??, hms.next()??, hms.next()??);
    let date = chrono::NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(year.parse().ok()?, month, day.parse().ok()?)?;
    Some(date.and_hms_opt(h, m, sec)?.and_utc().timestamp())
}

pub struct SystemProc;

impl ProcCheck for SystemProc {
    fn check(&self, pid: i32, proc_start: &str) -> ProcVerdict {
        let Some(claimed) = parse_lstart_utc(proc_start) else {
            return ProcVerdict::Unverifiable;
        };
        match process_start_secs(pid) {
            Ok(Some(actual)) if (actual - claimed).abs() <= 1 => ProcVerdict::Live,
            Ok(_) => ProcVerdict::Gone,
            Err(()) => ProcVerdict::Unverifiable,
        }
    }
}

fn pid_exists(pid: i32) -> Option<bool> {
    // SAFETY: signal 0 only checks for existence and permission.
    if unsafe { libc::kill(pid, 0) } == 0 {
        return Some(true);
    }
    match std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error() {
        Some(libc::ESRCH) => Some(false),
        Some(libc::EPERM) => Some(true),
        _ => None,
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
fn process_start_secs(pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    match pid_exists(pid) {
        Some(false) => return Ok(None),
        None => return Err(()),
        Some(true) => {}
    }
    let mut info: libc::proc_bsdinfo = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
    let size = std::mem::size_of::<libc::proc_bsdinfo>() as libc::c_int;
    // SAFETY: `info` is a properly sized, writable proc_bsdinfo.
    let n = unsafe {
        libc::proc_pidinfo(pid, libc::PROC_PIDTBSDINFO, 0, &mut info as *mut _ as *mut libc::c_void, size)
    };
    if n == size { Ok(Some(info.pbi_start_tvsec as i64)) } else { Err(()) }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn process_start_secs(pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    let stat = match std::fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{pid}/stat")) {
        Ok(s) => s,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
        Err(_) => return Err(()),
    };
    // Field 22 (starttime, clock ticks after boot); fields after the ")"
    // that ends the command name start at field 3.
    let after = stat.rsplit_once(')').ok_or(())?.1;
    let ticks: i64 = after.split_whitespace().nth(19).ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;
    let btime: i64 = std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/stat")
        .map_err(|_| ())?
        .lines()
        .find_map(|l| l.strip_prefix("btime "))
        .ok_or(())?
        .trim()
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| ())?;
    // SAFETY: sysconf has no preconditions.
    let hz = unsafe { libc::sysconf(libc::_SC_CLK_TCK) };
    if hz <= 0 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok(Some(btime + ticks / hz as i64))
}

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "macos", target_os = "linux")))]
fn process_start_secs(_pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    Err(())
}

pub struct Scan {
    pub live: Vec<RegistryEntry>,
    /// False when the directory or any entry could not be read or
    /// verified. An incomplete scan never removes an agent (spec §6.2).
    pub complete: bool,
}

pub struct Scanner {
    dir: PathBuf,
    proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    logged: HashSet<String>,
}

impl Scanner {
    pub fn new(dir: PathBuf, proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>) -> Scanner {
        Scanner { dir, proc, logged: HashSet::new() }
    }

    fn log_once(&mut self, what: &str, why: &str) {
        if self.logged.insert(format!("{what}:{why}")) {
            tracing::info!(entry = %what, %why, "skipping a Claude session registry entry");
        }
    }

    pub fn scan(&mut self) -> Scan {
        let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(&self.dir) {
            Ok(e) => e,
            Err(_) => {
                self.log_once("directory", "unreadable");
                return Scan { live: vec![], complete: false };
            }
        };
        let mut live = Vec::new();
        let mut complete = true;
        for e in entries.flatten() {
            let name = e.file_name();
            let Some(name) = name.to_str() else { continue };
            let Some(stem) = name.strip_suffix(".json") else { continue };
            if stem.is_empty() || !stem.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
                continue;
            }
            let entry = acd_securefs::read_private(&self.dir.join(name), MAX_ENTRY_MODE, MAX_ENTRY_BYTES)
                .ok()
                .and_then(|b| serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&b).ok())
                .and_then(|v| RegistryEntry::from_value(&v))
                .filter(|e| e.pid.to_string() == stem);
            let Some(entry) = entry else {
                complete = false;
                self.log_once(name, "unreadable");
                continue;
            };
            match entry.proc_start.as_deref().map(|p| self.proc.check(entry.pid, p)) {
                Some(ProcVerdict::Live) => live.push(entry),
                Some(ProcVerdict::Gone) => {}
                Some(ProcVerdict::Unverifiable) | None => {
                    complete = false;
                    self.log_once(name, "process metadata unverifiable");
                }
            }
        }
        Scan { live, complete }
    }
}

pub fn map_status(status: Option<&str>) -> AgentState {
    match status {
        Some("idle") => AgentState::Idle,
        Some("busy") => AgentState::Working,
        Some("waiting") => AgentState::NeedsYou,
        _ => AgentState::Unknown,
    }
}

pub fn sessions_dir(home: &Path, claude_config_dir: Option<&Path>) -> PathBuf {
    claude_config_dir.map(Path::to_path_buf).unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".claude")).join("sessions")
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::collections::HashMap;

    struct T3(HashMap<String, String>);
    impl SessionOwners for T3 {
        fn owner(&self, s: &str) -> Option<String> { self.0.get(s).cloned() }
    }

    fn pane(id: &str, kind: AgentKind, session: Option<&str>, cwd: Option<&str>) -> PaneFact {
        PaneFact {
            agent_id: format!("herdr:{}:{id}", socket_hash(Path::new("/h/.config/herdr/herdr.sock"))),
            socket: "/h/.config/herdr/herdr.sock".into(),
            pane_id: id.into(),
            kind,
            session: session.map(Into::into),
            cwd: cwd.map(Into::into),
        }
    }
    fn hints(pane: &str, socket: &str) -> Hints {
        Hints { herdr_pane: Some(pane.into()), herdr_socket: Some(socket.into()), entrypoint: None }
    }
    const SOCK: &str = "/h/.config/herdr/herdr.sock";

    #[test]
    fn t3_wins_over_every_other_rule() {
        let t3 = T3([("s".to_string(), "t3:th".to_string())].into());
        let panes = [pane("w1:p1", AgentKind::Claude, Some("s"), None)];
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &hints("w1:p1", SOCK), Some("cli"), Some(&t3), &panes, &[]), Owner::T3("t3:th".into()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn header_binding_needs_a_known_socket_and_a_claude_pane() {
        let panes = [pane("w1:p1", AgentKind::Claude, None, None), pane("w1:p2", AgentKind::Codex, None, None)];
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &hints("w1:p1", SOCK), None, None, &panes, &[]), Owner::Herdr(panes[0].agent_id.clone()));
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &hints("w1:p1", "/other/herdr.sock"), None, None, &panes, &[]), Owner::Hooks,
            "a pane id from another herdr session must not bind (ids repeat across sessions)");
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &hints("w1:p2", SOCK), None, None, &panes, &[]), Owner::Hooks, "not a claude pane");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_header_pane_reporting_another_session_does_not_bind() {
        let panes = [pane("w1:p1", AgentKind::Claude, Some("other"), None)];
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &hints("w1:p1", SOCK), None, None, &panes, &[]), Owner::Hooks);
    }

    #[test]
    fn agent_session_binds_without_headers() {
        let panes = [pane("w1:p1", AgentKind::Claude, Some("s"), None)];
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &Hints::default(), None, None, &panes, &[]), Owner::Herdr(panes[0].agent_id.clone()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn ignored_entrypoint_is_unknown_unless_bound() {
        let ignored = vec!["sdk-ts".to_string()];
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &Hints::default(), Some("sdk-ts"), None, &[], &ignored), Owner::Unknown);
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &Hints::default(), Some("cli"), None, &[], &ignored), Owner::Hooks);
        assert_eq!(claude_owner("s", &Hints::default(), None, None, &[], &ignored), Owner::Hooks, "an unset entrypoint is not ignored");
        let panes = [pane("w1:p1", AgentKind::Claude, Some("s"), None)];
        assert!(matches!(claude_owner("s", &Hints::default(), Some("sdk-ts"), None, &panes, &ignored), Owner::Herdr(_)),
            "a herdr binding keeps a session whatever its inherited entrypoint (P0 finding 2)");
    }

    #[test]
    fn codex_env_is_never_used_for_binding() {
        // The signature has no hints: gate 4 showed the env names a stale or
        // other pane. Two panes in one cwd stay ambiguous whatever the env says.
        let panes = [pane("w1:p1", AgentKind::Codex, None, Some("/p")), pane("w1:p2", AgentKind::Codex, None, Some("/p"))];
        assert_eq!(codex_bind("s", Some("/p"), &panes), CodexBind::Ambiguous);
    }

    #[test]
    fn codex_binds_by_session_then_by_the_only_pane_in_the_cwd() {
        let a = pane("w1:p1", AgentKind::Codex, Some("s"), Some("/p"));
        let b = pane("w1:p2", AgentKind::Codex, None, Some("/p"));
        assert_eq!(codex_bind("s", Some("/p"), &[a.clone(), b.clone()]), CodexBind::Session(a.clone()));
        assert_eq!(codex_bind("s2", Some("/p"), &[a.clone(), b.clone()]), CodexBind::OnlyInCwd(b.clone()),
            "a pane reporting another session is not a candidate");
        assert_eq!(codex_bind("s2", Some("/q"), &[a, b]), CodexBind::Unbound);
        assert_eq!(codex_bind("s2", None, &[]), CodexBind::Unbound);
    }

    #[test]
    fn same_dir_resolves_symlinks() {
        let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let real = tmp.path().join("real");
        std::fs::create_dir(&real).unwrap();
        let link = tmp.path().join("link");
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&real, &link).unwrap();
        assert!(same_dir(link.to_str().unwrap(), real.to_str().unwrap()));
        assert!(same_dir("/nonexistent/x/", "/nonexistent/x"));
        assert!(!same_dir("/nonexistent/x", "/nonexistent/y"));
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Who owns a session (spec §6.2 Ownership) and which herdr pane it is
//! bound to (§6.2 Binding; §6.3 Binding as amended by gate 4).
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use acd_protocol::AgentKind;

use super::wire::{Hints, valid_id};
use crate::herdr::map::socket_hash;
use crate::herdr::reconciler::PaneMap;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PaneFact {
    pub agent_id: String,
    pub socket: PathBuf,
    pub pane_id: String,
    pub kind: AgentKind,
    pub session: Option<String>,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
}

pub fn pane_facts(panes: &PaneMap) -> Vec<PaneFact> {
    panes
        .read()
        .expect("pane map")
        .iter()
        .map(|(id, p)| PaneFact {
            agent_id: id.clone(),
            socket: p.socket.clone(),
            pane_id: p.pane_id.clone(),
            kind: p.kind,
            session: p.session.clone(),
            cwd: p.cwd.clone(),
        })
        .collect()
}

pub trait SessionOwners: Send + Sync {
    fn owner(&self, session_id: &str) -> Option<String>;
}

impl SessionOwners for SessionMap {
    fn owner(&self, session_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
        SessionMap::owner(self, session_id)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Owner {
    T3(String),
    Herdr(String),
    Hooks,
    Unknown,
}

/// The pane named by the Claude headers. The socket header is only
/// compared through `socket_hash` with sockets herdr discovery already
/// knows; acd never opens a path taken from a header. A pane that reports
/// a different session is not this session's pane.
pub fn header_pane<'a>(hints: &Hints, session_id: &str, panes: &'a [PaneFact]) -> Option<&'a PaneFact> {
    let pane = hints.herdr_pane.as_deref().filter(|p| valid_id(p))?;
    let hash = socket_hash(Path::new(hints.herdr_socket.as_deref()?));
    panes.iter().find(|f| {
        f.pane_id == pane
            && f.kind == AgentKind::Claude
            && socket_hash(&f.socket) == hash
            && f.session.as_deref().is_none_or(|s| s == session_id)
    })
}

/// Spec §6.2 Ownership, rules 1 to 4, in order.
pub fn claude_owner(
    session_id: &str,
    hints: &Hints,
    entrypoint: Option<&str>,
    t3: Option<&dyn SessionOwners>,
    panes: &[PaneFact],
    ignored: &[String],
) -> Owner {
    if let Some(agent) = t3.and_then(|m| m.owner(session_id)) {
        return Owner::T3(agent);
    }
    if let Some(p) = header_pane(hints, session_id, panes) {
        return Owner::Herdr(p.agent_id.clone());
    }
    if let Some(p) = panes.iter().find(|p| p.kind == AgentKind::Claude && p.session.as_deref() == Some(session_id)) {
        return Owner::Herdr(p.agent_id.clone());
    }
    match entrypoint {
        Some(e) if ignored.iter().any(|i| i == e) => Owner::Unknown,
        _ => Owner::Hooks,
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CodexBind {
    Session(PaneFact),
    OnlyInCwd(PaneFact),
    Ambiguous,
    Unbound,
}

/// Gate 4: the hook env is never used. A codex pane reporting this
/// session wins; otherwise the only codex pane in the cwd that does not
/// report another session. Either way the caller still requires that
/// pane's own screen to show the request's dialog before any key.
pub fn codex_bind(session_id: &str, cwd: Option<&str>, panes: &[PaneFact]) -> CodexBind {
    let codex: Vec<&PaneFact> = panes.iter().filter(|p| p.kind == AgentKind::Codex).collect();
    if let Some(p) = codex.iter().find(|p| p.session.as_deref() == Some(session_id)) {
        return CodexBind::Session((*p).clone());
    }
    let Some(cwd) = cwd else { return CodexBind::Unbound };
    let same: Vec<&&PaneFact> = codex
        .iter()
        .filter(|p| p.session.is_none() && p.cwd.as_deref().is_some_and(|c| same_dir(c, cwd)))
        .collect();
    match same.as_slice() {
        [] => CodexBind::Unbound,
        [one] => CodexBind::OnlyInCwd((**one).clone()),
        _ => CodexBind::Ambiguous,
    }
}

/// herdr reports `/tmp/...`, Codex `/private/tmp/...` (D5).
pub fn same_dir(a: &str, b: &str) -> bool {
    let canon = |s: &str| std::fs::canonicalize(s).unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from(s.trim_end_matches('/')));
    canon(a) == canon(b)
}
```

```rust
#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::{HerdrAdapter, HerdrConfig, fake::FakeHerdr, keys::KeysTiming, reconciler::Timing};
use acd_adapters::hooks::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, ProcVerdict};
use acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::HookAuth;
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::{post, send};
use acd_adapters::hooks::{HooksAdapter, HooksConfig};
use acd_adapters::t3::sessions::SessionMap;
use acd_core::{Core, testing::test_machine};
use acd_protocol::Agent;
use serde_json::Value;

pub const TOKEN: &str = "tok-hooks-test-0123456789";

pub struct Live;
impl ProcCheck for Live {
    fn check(&self, _: i32, _: &str) -> ProcVerdict { ProcVerdict::Live }
}

pub struct Rig {
    pub core: Arc<Core>,
    pub hooks: Arc<HooksAdapter>,
    pub herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    pub fake: Option<FakeHerdr>,
    pub t3: Arc<SessionMap>,
    pub port: u16,
    pub home: tempfile::TempDir,
}

pub fn fast(cfg: &mut HooksConfig) {
    cfg.tick = Duration::from_millis(20);
    cfg.scan_interval = Duration::from_millis(100);
    cfg.evidence_window = Duration::from_millis(400);
    cfg.capture_window = Duration::from_millis(600);
    cfg.capture_poll = Duration::from_millis(50);
    cfg.codex_window = Duration::from_millis(600);
    cfg.held_dialog_poll = Duration::from_millis(100);
    cfg.end_grace = Duration::from_millis(200);
}

/// `panes`: herdr agents to fake; empty means no herdr at all.
pub async fn rig(panes: &[Value], tweak: impl FnOnce(&mut HooksConfig)) -> Rig {
    let home = tempfile::Builder::new().prefix("acd").tempdir_in("/tmp").unwrap();
    let (herdr, fake) = if panes.is_empty() {
        (None, None)
    } else {
        let sock = home.path().join(".config/herdr/herdr.sock");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(sock.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        let fake = FakeHerdr::start_at(&sock).await;
        for p in panes { fake.set_agent(p.clone()); }
        let cfg = HerdrConfig {
            enabled: true, home: home.path().into(), env_socket: None, extra_sockets: vec![],
            scan_interval: Duration::from_millis(100),
            timing: Timing { list_idle: Duration::from_millis(300), list_blocked: Duration::from_millis(100),
                             backoff_max: Duration::from_millis(200), fallback_after: Duration::from_millis(200),
                             poll: Duration::from_millis(50) },
            keys: KeysTiming { evidence_timeout: Duration::from_millis(800), ..KeysTiming::default() },
        };
        (Some(HerdrAdapter::new(cfg)), Some(fake))
    };
    let sessions = home.path().join(".claude/sessions");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&sessions).unwrap();
    let mut cfg = HooksConfig::new(Duration::from_secs(3600), vec!["sdk-ts".into()], Some(sessions));
    fast(&mut cfg);
    tweak(&mut cfg);
    let t3 = Arc::new(SessionMap::default());
    let hooks = HooksAdapter::new(cfg, herdr.clone(), Some(t3.clone()), Arc::new(Live));
    let mut adapters: Vec<Arc<dyn acd_core::Adapter>> = vec![hooks.clone()];
    if let Some(h) = &herdr { adapters.push(h.clone()); }
    let core = Core::new(test_machine(), adapters);
    if let Some(h) = &herdr {
        // h.set_evidence(hooks.evidence()); // Task 12 adds HooksAdapter::evidence and restores this line
        h.start(core.clone());
    }
    hooks.start(core.clone());
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let app = hooks.router(Arc::new(HookAuth::new(TOKEN.into())));
    tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap() });
    if let Some(h) = &herdr {
        let n = panes.len();
        until("herdr panes", || (h.panes().read().unwrap().len() == n).then_some(())).await;
    }
    Rig { core, hooks, herdr, fake, t3, port, home }
}

pub async fn until<T>(what: &str, mut f: impl FnMut() -> Option<T>) -> T {
    for _ in 0..250 {
        if let Some(v) = f() { return v; }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
    }
    panic!("timed out waiting for {what}");
}

pub fn fx(rel: &str) -> Value {
    let p = format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"));
    serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

pub const SID: &str = "4e3885ee-7454-410d-a2e2-86f4dc2533c0";

impl Rig {
    pub fn sock(&self) -> String { self.fake.as_ref().unwrap().path().to_string_lossy().into_owned() }
    pub async fn hook(&self, agent: &str, event: &str, body: &Value, headers: &[(&str, &str)]) -> (u16, String) {
        post(self.port, &format!("/hook/{agent}/{event}"), TOKEN, headers, &body.to_string()).await
    }
    pub async fn hold(&self, body: &Value, headers: &[(&str, &str)]) -> tokio::net::TcpStream {
        send(self.port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, headers, &body.to_string()).await
    }
    pub fn agent(&self, id: &str) -> Option<Agent> { self.core.agent(id) }
}

pub const CLI: [(&str, &str); 1] = [("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")];
```

```rust
    until("pane session", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().values().find_map(|p| p.session.clone())).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("migrated", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none().then_some(())).await;
}
```

```rust
//! Claude and Codex hooks (spec §6.1 to §6.3).
pub mod claude_sessions;
pub mod endpoint;
pub mod mapping;
pub mod owner;
mod state;
#[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]
pub mod testing;
pub mod wire;

use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_core::{Adapter, Answer, Core, DeliverError};
use acd_protocol::{Agent, AnswerPath, ErrorCode, Request, Source};
use async_trait::async_trait;
use tokio::sync::mpsc;

use self::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, RegistryEntry};
use self::endpoint::{EndpointOptions, HookAuth, Inbound};
use crate::herdr::HerdrAdapter;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct HooksConfig {
    /// `claude.permission_hold` (spec §9); holds end here with an empty reply.
    pub permission_hold: Duration,
    pub ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>,
    /// `~/.claude/sessions`; `None` disables the registry scan.
    pub sessions_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub scan_interval: Duration,
    pub tick: Duration,
    /// Correlated outcome evidence window (spec §5.1, §6.2: 30 s).
    pub evidence_window: Duration,
    /// Fingerprint capture after a request opens (spec §6.2 step 6: 2 s).
    pub capture_window: Duration,
    pub capture_poll: Duration,
    /// Codex dialog wait (spec §6.3: 1 s).
    pub codex_window: Duration,
    /// Presence checks of a held request's captured dialog.
    pub held_dialog_poll: Duration,
    /// Removal after `SessionEnd` or the registry entry disappearing (10 min).
    pub end_grace: Duration,
    /// Removal after no event at all (24 h).
    pub idle_expiry: Duration,
    pub max_holds: usize,
}

impl HooksConfig {
    pub fn new(permission_hold: Duration, ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>, sessions_dir: Option<PathBuf>) -> HooksConfig {
        HooksConfig {
            permission_hold,
            ignored_entrypoints,
            sessions_dir,
            scan_interval: Duration::from_secs(30),
            tick: Duration::from_millis(250),
            evidence_window: Duration::from_secs(30),
            capture_window: Duration::from_secs(2),
            capture_poll: Duration::from_millis(200),
            codex_window: Duration::from_secs(1),
            held_dialog_poll: Duration::from_secs(1),
            end_grace: Duration::from_secs(600),
            idle_expiry: Duration::from_secs(24 * 3600),
            max_holds: 64,
        }
    }
}

pub struct HooksAdapter {
    cfg: HooksConfig,
    herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
    proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    shared: Arc<state::Shared>,
    tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>,
    rx: Mutex<Option<mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Inbound>>>,
}

impl HooksAdapter {
    pub fn new(
        cfg: HooksConfig,
        herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
        t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
        proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    ) -> Arc<HooksAdapter> {
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        Arc::new(HooksAdapter { cfg, herdr, t3, proc, shared: Arc::default(), tx, rx: Mutex::new(Some(rx)) })
    }

    pub fn router(&self, auth: Arc<HookAuth>) -> axum::Router {
        endpoint::router(
            auth,
            self.tx.clone(),
            EndpointOptions { permission_hold: self.cfg.permission_hold, max_holds: self.cfg.max_holds, ..Default::default() },
        )
    }

    /// Spawns the reconciler. Call once.
    pub fn start(self: &Arc<Self>, core: Arc<Core>) {
        let rx = self.rx.lock().expect("rx").take().expect("HooksAdapter::start called twice");
        let r = state::Reconciler::new(
            self.cfg.clone(), core, self.herdr.clone(), self.t3.clone(), self.proc.clone(), self.shared.clone(), rx,
        );
        tokio::spawn(r.run());
    }

    pub fn live_sessions(&self) -> Option<Vec<RegistryEntry>> {
        self.shared.live_sessions.lock().expect("shared").clone()
    }

    pub fn current_token_sessions(&self) -> HashSet<String> {
        self.shared.current_token.lock().expect("shared").clone()
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl Adapter for HooksAdapter {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "hooks"
    }
    fn answer_paths(&self) -> &[AnswerPath] {
        &[AnswerPath::Hook]
    }
    fn sources(&self) -> &[Source] {
        &[Source::Hooks]
    }
    async fn deliver(&self, _request: &Request, _answer: &Answer) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone)) // Task 11b
    }
    /// Hooks-only agents have `can_send: false`; the core refuses first.
    async fn send(&self, _agent: &Agent, _text: &str) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::NotSupported))
    }
}
```

`hooks/state.rs`:

```rust
//! The hooks reconciler: the only code that changes hook-derived state.
//! It drains the endpoint's mailbox in arrival order and its own internal
//! channel (results of spawned screen reads and scans). It never awaits a
//! herdr call itself.
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use acd_core::{AgentObs, Core, Observation};
use acd_protocol::{AgentKind, AgentState, Effect, Source};
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot, watch};
use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::HooksConfig;
use super::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, RegistryEntry, Scan, Scanner, map_status};
use super::endpoint::{HoldId, Inbound, TokenUse};
use super::owner::{Owner, PaneFact, claude_owner, pane_facts};
use super::wire::{AlwaysRule, HookAgent, HookEvent, HookName};
use crate::herdr::HerdrAdapter;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;
use crate::text::{LAST_MESSAGE_CHARS, TITLE_CHARS, cap_chars, clean_display};

/// State `deliver` and the reconciler share. `deliver` only takes a reply
/// sender and stamps `delivered`; no lock is held across an await.
#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Shared {
    pub held: Mutex<HashMap<String, HeldReq>>,
    pub live_sessions: Mutex<Option<Vec<RegistryEntry>>>,
    pub current_token: Mutex<HashSet<String>>,
}

pub(crate) struct HeldReq {
    pub hold: HoldId,
    pub reply: Option<oneshot::Sender<String>>,
    pub rule: Option<AlwaysRule>,
    pub delivered: Option<(Effect, Instant)>,
}

pub(crate) enum Internal {
    Scanned(Scan),
}

struct Session {
    owner: Owner,
    /// The registry agent this session shows as, if any.
    agent_id: Option<String>,
    hooks_only: bool,
    cwd: Option<String>,
    name: Option<String>,
    entrypoint: Option<String>,
    state: AgentState,
    last_message: Option<String>,
    last_hook: Option<Instant>,
    last_event: Instant,
    in_registry: bool,
    ended: Option<Instant>,
}

pub(crate) struct Reconciler {
    cfg: HooksConfig,
    core: Arc<Core>,
    herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    inbound: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Inbound>,
    internal_tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Internal>,
    internal_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Internal>,
    scanner: Option<Arc<Mutex<Scanner>>>,
    sessions: HashMap<String, Session>,
}

impl Reconciler {
    pub(crate) fn new(
        cfg: HooksConfig,
        core: Arc<Core>,
        herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
        t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
        proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
        shared: Arc<Shared>,
        inbound: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Inbound>,
    ) -> Reconciler {
        let (internal_tx, internal_rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        let scanner = cfg.sessions_dir.clone().map(|d| Arc::new(Mutex::new(Scanner::new(d, proc))));
        Reconciler { cfg, core, herdr, t3, shared, inbound, internal_tx, internal_rx, scanner, sessions: HashMap::new() }
    }

    pub(crate) async fn run(mut self) {
        let mut tick = tokio::time::interval(self.cfg.tick);
        let mut t3_changes = self.t3.as_ref().map(|m| m.changes());
        let mut next_scan = Instant::now();
        loop {
            tokio::select! {
                biased;
                Some(m) = self.internal_rx.recv() => self.on_internal(m).await,
                m = self.inbound.recv() => match m {
                    Some(m) => self.on_inbound(m).await,
                    None => return,
                },
                _ = changed(&mut t3_changes) => self.reevaluate_all().await,
                _ = tick.tick() => {
                    let now = Instant::now();
                    if self.scanner.is_some() && now >= next_scan {
                        next_scan = now + self.cfg.scan_interval;
                        self.spawn_scan();
                    }
                    self.on_tick().await;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn spawn_scan(&self) {
        let Some(scanner) = self.scanner.clone() else { return };
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
            let scan = scanner.lock().expect("scanner").scan();
            let _ = tx.send(Internal::Scanned(scan));
        });
    }

    fn panes(&self) -> Vec<PaneFact> {
        self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| pane_facts(&h.panes())).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    fn owner_of(&self, session_id: &str, hints: &super::wire::Hints, entrypoint: Option<&str>) -> Owner {
        let t3 = self.t3.as_deref().map(|m| m as &dyn super::owner::SessionOwners);
        claude_owner(session_id, hints, entrypoint, t3, &self.panes(), &self.cfg.ignored_entrypoints)
    }

    async fn on_inbound(&mut self, m: Inbound) {
        match m {
            Inbound::Event { event, token } => self.on_event(event, token, None).await,
            Inbound::Held { event, hold, reply, token } => self.on_event(event, token, Some((hold, reply))).await,
            Inbound::HoldClosed { hold } => self.on_hold_ended(hold, false).await,
            Inbound::HoldExpired { hold } => self.on_hold_ended(hold, true).await,
        }
    }

    async fn on_event(&mut self, event: HookEvent, token: TokenUse, hold: Option<(HoldId, oneshot::Sender<String>)>) {
        if token == TokenUse::Current {
            self.shared.current_token.lock().expect("shared").insert(event.payload.session_id.clone());
        }
        if event.payload.is_subagent() {
            return; // `hold` drops here: the endpoint replies empty
        }
        match event.agent {
            HookAgent::Claude => self.on_claude(event, hold).await,
            HookAgent::Codex => self.on_codex(event).await,
        }
    }

    async fn on_claude(&mut self, event: HookEvent, hold: Option<(HoldId, oneshot::Sender<String>)>) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let entrypoint = event.hints.entrypoint.clone();
        let owner = self.owner_of(&sid, &event.hints, entrypoint.as_deref());
        let now = Instant::now();
        let s = self.sessions.entry(sid.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Session {
            owner: Owner::Unknown, agent_id: None, hooks_only: false, cwd: None, name: None, entrypoint: None,
            state: AgentState::Idle, last_message: None, last_hook: None, last_event: now, in_registry: false, ended: None,
        });
        s.last_event = now;
        s.last_hook = Some(now);
        if event.payload.cwd.is_some() {
            s.cwd = event.payload.cwd.clone();
        }
        if entrypoint.is_some() {
            s.entrypoint = entrypoint;
        }
        match event.name {
            HookName::UserPromptSubmit => s.state = AgentState::Working,
            HookName::Stop => {
                s.state = AgentState::Done;
                s.last_message = event
                    .payload
                    .last_assistant_message
                    .as_deref()
                    .map(|m| cap_chars(&clean_display(m, usize::MAX), LAST_MESSAGE_CHARS));
            }
            HookName::SessionEnd => s.ended = Some(now),
            _ => {}
        }
        self.apply_owner(&sid, owner).await;
        let _ = hold; // permission requests: Task 11b
    }

    async fn on_codex(&mut self, _event: HookEvent) {} // Task 13

    async fn on_hold_ended(&mut self, _hold: HoldId, _expired: bool) {} // Task 11c

    async fn on_internal(&mut self, m: Internal) {
        match m {
            Internal::Scanned(scan) => self.on_scan(scan).await,
        }
    }

    /// Moves a session to `owner`, publishing whatever that changes.
    async fn apply_owner(&mut self, sid: &str, owner: Owner) {
        let hooks_id = format!("claude:{sid}");
        let Some(s) = self.sessions.get_mut(sid) else { return };
        let was_hooks_only = s.hooks_only;
        s.owner = owner.clone();
        match owner {
            Owner::T3(_) | Owner::Unknown => {
                s.agent_id = None;
                s.hooks_only = false;
                if was_hooks_only {
                    self.core.observe(Observation::AgentGone { agent_id: hooks_id });
                }
            }
            Owner::Herdr(agent) => {
                s.agent_id = Some(agent.clone());
                s.hooks_only = false;
                if was_hooks_only && self.core.agent(&agent).is_some() {
                    let lock = self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| h.agent_locks());
                    let _g = match &lock {
                        Some(l) => Some(l.lock(&agent).await),
                        None => None,
                    };
                    self.core.observe(Observation::AgentMigrated { from: hooks_id, to: agent });
                }
            }
            Owner::Hooks => {
                s.agent_id = Some(hooks_id.clone());
                s.hooks_only = true;
                let obs = hooks_only_obs(&hooks_id, s);
                self.core.observe(Observation::AgentUpsert(obs));
            }
        }
    }

    async fn reevaluate_all(&mut self) {
        let ids: Vec<(String, Option<String>)> =
            self.sessions.iter().map(|(k, s)| (k.clone(), s.entrypoint.clone())).collect();
        for (sid, entrypoint) in ids {
            let owner = self.owner_of(&sid, &Default::default(), entrypoint.as_deref());
            let changed = self.sessions.get(&sid).is_some_and(|s| s.owner != owner);
            if changed {
                self.apply_owner(&sid, owner).await;
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_scan(&mut self, scan: Scan) {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let live: HashSet<String> = scan.live.iter().map(|e| e.session_id.clone()).collect();
        for e in &scan.live {
            let s = self.sessions.entry(e.session_id.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Session {
                owner: Owner::Unknown, agent_id: None, hooks_only: false, cwd: None, name: None, entrypoint: None,
                state: AgentState::Unknown, last_message: None, last_hook: None, last_event: now, in_registry: true, ended: None,
            });
            s.in_registry = true;
            s.last_event = now;
            s.cwd = s.cwd.clone().or_else(|| e.cwd.clone());
            s.name = e.name.clone().or(s.name.take());
            if s.entrypoint.is_none() {
                s.entrypoint = e.entrypoint.clone();
            }
            // D11: hooks are fresher than the scan, and done stays done.
            let fresh_hook = s.last_hook.is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(t) < self.cfg.scan_interval);
            let scanned = map_status(e.status.as_deref());
            if !fresh_hook && !(s.state == AgentState::Done && scanned == AgentState::Idle) {
                s.state = scanned;
            }
        }
        if scan.complete {
            for (sid, s) in self.sessions.iter_mut() {
                if s.in_registry && !live.contains(sid) && s.ended.is_none() {
                    s.ended = Some(now);
                }
            }
            *self.shared.live_sessions.lock().expect("shared") = Some(scan.live.clone());
        }
        for e in &scan.live {
            let owner = self.owner_of(&e.session_id, &Default::default(), e.entrypoint.as_deref());
            self.apply_owner(&e.session_id, owner).await;
        }
    }

    async fn on_tick(&mut self) {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let gone: Vec<String> = self
            .sessions
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, s)| {
                s.ended.is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(t) >= self.cfg.end_grace)
                    || now.duration_since(s.last_event) >= self.cfg.idle_expiry
            })
            .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
            .collect();
        for sid in gone {
            if let Some(s) = self.sessions.remove(&sid)
                && s.hooks_only
            {
                self.core.observe(Observation::AgentGone { agent_id: format!("claude:{sid}") });
            }
        }
    }
}

fn hooks_only_obs(id: &str, s: &Session) -> AgentObs {
    let project = s
        .cwd
        .as_deref()
        .and_then(|c| Path::new(c).file_name())
        .map(|n| clean_display(&n.to_string_lossy(), TITLE_CHARS))
        .filter(|p| !p.is_empty());
    let title = s
        .name
        .as_deref()
        .map(|n| clean_display(n, TITLE_CHARS))
        .filter(|t| !t.is_empty())
        .or_else(|| project.clone())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "Claude".into());
    AgentObs {
        id: id.into(),
        source: Source::Hooks,
        kind: AgentKind::Claude,
        title,
        cwd: s.cwd.as_deref().map(|c| clean_display(c, usize::MAX)),
        project,
        state: s.state,
        failed: false,
        last_message: s.last_message.clone(),
        can_send: false,
    }
}

async fn changed(rx: &mut Option<watch::Receiver<u64>>) {
    match rx {
        Some(r) if r.changed().await.is_ok() => {}
        _ => std::future::pending().await,
    }
}
```

Note: `apply_owner` re-upserts a hooks-only agent on every event, which
is how state changes (`working`, `done`) reach the registry; the
registry drops identical upserts.
