### Task 11a: Outcome rules and hook-to-dialog matching

Pure modules used by Tasks 11b to 13: what a later hook event says about
a request (spec §5.1 Attribution, §6.2 step 3, §6.3 Completion), and
which on-screen dialog a hook belongs to (§6.2 step 6, §6.3). They touch
no state, so this task can run in parallel with Task 10.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/evidence.rs`
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/dialogs.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (module list)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6 (`HookEvent`, `HookName`, `tool_digest`, `command_of`),
  herdr `screen::find_dialog` and `keys::Verdict`.
- Produces:
  - `evidence::Expect { ClaudeTool { session_id, tool_name, digest }, ClaudeQuestion { session_id, question: String, labels: HashMap<String, String> }, CodexTool { session_id, tool_name, command } }`.
  - `evidence::Seen { ToolRan, TurnEnded, Answered(String) }`,
    `evidence::Sent { Allow, Deny, Choose(String) }`,
    `evidence::observe(expect: &Expect, ev: &HookEvent) -> Option<Seen>`,
    `evidence::verdict(sent: &Sent, seen: &Seen) -> Verdict` (herdr's `keys::Verdict`),
    `evidence::sent_for_effect(Effect) -> Option<Sent>`.
  - `dialogs::DialogMatch { ClaudePermission { needle: String }, ClaudeQuestion { labels: Vec<String> }, CodexPermission { needle: String } }`,
    `DialogMatch::find(&self, screen: &str) -> Option<Dialog>`,
    `DialogMatch::collapsed_codex(screen: &str) -> bool`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing unit tests**

`dialogs.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn screen(name: &str) -> String {
        std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_claude_permission_matches_its_command_only() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt");
        assert!(DialogMatch::ClaudePermission { needle: "touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt".into() }.find(&s).is_some());
        assert!(DialogMatch::ClaudePermission { needle: "touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt".into() }.find(&s).is_none());
        assert!(DialogMatch::CodexPermission { needle: "touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt".into() }.find(&s).is_none(), "wrong dialog kind");
        assert!(DialogMatch::ClaudePermission { needle: String::new() }.find(&s).is_none(), "an empty needle never matches");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrapped_commands_still_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt").replace(" touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt", " touch /tmp/ac\n p0/t1.txt");
        assert!(DialogMatch::ClaudePermission { needle: "touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt".into() }.find(&s).is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn codex_and_question_dialogs_match() {
        assert!(DialogMatch::CodexPermission { needle: "touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt".into() }
            .find(&screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt")).is_some());
        let q = screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt");
        assert!(DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { labels: vec!["Red".into(), "Blue".into()] }.find(&q).is_some());
        assert!(DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { labels: vec!["Blue".into(), "Red".into()] }.find(&q).is_none());
        assert!(DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { labels: vec!["Red".into()] }
            .find(&screen("claude-askuserquestion-multi.txt")).is_none(), "a tab strip is never matched");
        assert!(DialogMatch::collapsed_codex(&screen("codex-permission-collapsed-small-pane-detection.txt")));
    }
}
```

`evidence.rs`:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::hooks::wire::{HookAgent, HookName, Hints, Payload};

    fn ev(rel: &str, name: HookName) -> HookEvent {
        let p = format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"));
        let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap();
        HookEvent { agent: HookAgent::Claude, name, payload: Payload::from_value(&v).unwrap(), hints: Hints::default(),
                    at: tokio::time::Instant::now() }
    }
    fn bash(command: &str) -> Expect {
        let input = serde_json::json!({"command": command, "description": "Create empty file"});
        Expect::ClaudeTool { session_id: "4e3885ee-7454-410d-a2e2-86f4dc2533c0".into(), tool_name: "Bash".into(),
                             digest: crate::hooks::wire::tool_digest("Bash", &input) }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn claude_allow_and_deny_follow_section_6_2() {
        let post = ev("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json", HookName::PostToolUse); // t2.txt
        let stop = ev("claude/payload-Stop.json", HookName::Stop);
        let e = bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt");
        assert_eq!(observe(&e, &post), Some(Seen::ToolRan));
        assert_eq!(observe(&bash("touch /tmp/acp0/other"), &post), None, "another tool call is no evidence");
        assert_eq!(observe(&e, &stop), Some(Seen::TurnEnded));
        assert_eq!(verdict(&Sent::Allow, &Seen::ToolRan), Verdict::Consistent);
        assert_eq!(verdict(&Sent::Allow, &Seen::TurnEnded), Verdict::Contradicts);
        assert_eq!(verdict(&Sent::Deny, &Seen::TurnEnded), Verdict::Consistent);
        assert_eq!(verdict(&Sent::Deny, &Seen::ToolRan), Verdict::Contradicts);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn other_sessions_are_never_evidence() {
        let mut stop = ev("claude/payload-Stop.json", HookName::Stop);
        stop.payload.session_id = "someone-else".into();
        assert_eq!(observe(&bash("x"), &stop), None);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn questions_compare_the_answer_label() {
        let post = ev("claude/payload-PostToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json", HookName::PostToolUse);
        let e = Expect::ClaudeQuestion {
            session_id: "4e3885ee-7454-410d-a2e2-86f4dc2533c0".into(), question: "Pick a color".into(),
            labels: [("o1".to_string(), "Red".to_string()), ("o2".to_string(), "Blue".to_string())].into(),
        };
        assert_eq!(observe(&e, &post), Some(Seen::Answered("Red".into())));
        assert_eq!(verdict(&Sent::Choose("Red".into()), &Seen::Answered("Red".into())), Verdict::Consistent);
        assert_eq!(verdict(&Sent::Choose("Blue".into()), &Seen::Answered("Red".into())), Verdict::Contradicts);
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Matching a hook to the dialog on a pane (spec §6.2 step 6, §6.3). This
//! decides only which dialog a hook belongs to; the keys guard still
//! compares the full screen digest (`KeysTarget::from_screen`).
use crate::herdr::screen::{Dialog, DialogKind, find_dialog};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum DialogMatch {
    ClaudePermission { needle: String },
    ClaudeQuestion { labels: Vec<String> },
    CodexPermission { needle: String },
}

fn squash(s: &str) -> String {
    s.chars().filter(|c| !c.is_whitespace()).collect()
}

impl DialogMatch {
    pub fn find(&self, screen: &str) -> Option<Dialog> {
        let d = find_dialog(screen)?;
        let ok = match (self, &d.kind) {
            (DialogMatch::ClaudePermission { needle }, DialogKind::ClaudePermission)
            | (DialogMatch::CodexPermission { needle }, DialogKind::CodexPermission) => {
                let n = squash(needle);
                !n.is_empty() && squash(&d.text).contains(&n)
            }
            (DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { labels }, DialogKind::ClaudeQuestion { tabs: false }) => {
                !labels.is_empty()
                    && d.options.len() >= labels.len()
                    && labels.iter().zip(&d.options).all(|(l, o)| o.label.trim() == l.trim())
            }
            _ => false,
        };
        ok.then_some(d)
    }

    pub fn collapsed_codex(screen: &str) -> bool {
        find_dialog(screen).is_some_and(|d| d.kind == DialogKind::CodexCollapsed)
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Correlated outcome evidence (spec §5.1 Attribution, §6.2 step 3, §6.3
//! Completion). Pure rules here; `EvidenceHub` (Task 12) feeds the keys
//! engine with them.
use std::collections::HashMap;

use acd_protocol::Effect;
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::{HookEvent, HookName, command_of, tool_digest};
pub use crate::herdr::keys::Verdict;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Expect {
    ClaudeTool { session_id: String, tool_name: String, digest: String },
    ClaudeQuestion { session_id: String, question: String, labels: HashMap<String, String> },
    CodexTool { session_id: String, tool_name: String, command: String },
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Seen {
    ToolRan,
    TurnEnded,
    Answered(String),
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Sent {
    Allow,
    Deny,
    Choose(String),
}

pub fn sent_for_effect(e: Effect) -> Option<Sent> {
    match e {
        Effect::Allow | Effect::AllowAlways => Some(Sent::Allow),
        Effect::Deny => Some(Sent::Deny),
        Effect::Choose | Effect::Unknown => None,
    }
}

fn session_of(e: &Expect) -> &str {
    match e {
        Expect::ClaudeTool { session_id, .. }
        | Expect::ClaudeQuestion { session_id, .. }
        | Expect::CodexTool { session_id, .. } => session_id,
    }
}

/// What `ev` says about the expected request, if anything.
pub fn observe(expect: &Expect, ev: &HookEvent) -> Option<Seen> {
    if ev.payload.session_id != session_of(expect) {
        return None;
    }
    let turn_end = matches!(ev.name, HookName::Stop | HookName::UserPromptSubmit);
    let tool = ev.payload.tool_name.as_deref();
    match expect {
        Expect::ClaudeTool { tool_name, digest, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse
                && tool == Some(tool_name.as_str())
                && tool_digest(tool_name, &ev.payload.tool_input) == *digest
            {
                Some(Seen::ToolRan)
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
        Expect::ClaudeQuestion { question, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse && tool == Some("AskUserQuestion") {
                let answer = ev.payload.tool_response.get("answers").or_else(|| ev.payload.tool_input.get("answers"))
                    .and_then(|a| a.get(question.as_str()))
                    .and_then(Value::as_str)
                    .unwrap_or_default();
                Some(Seen::Answered(answer.to_owned()))
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
        Expect::CodexTool { tool_name, command, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse
                && tool == Some(tool_name.as_str())
                && command_of(&ev.payload.tool_input) == Some(command.as_str())
            {
                Some(Seen::ToolRan)
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
    }
}

pub fn verdict(sent: &Sent, seen: &Seen) -> Verdict {
    match (sent, seen) {
        (Sent::Allow, Seen::ToolRan) | (Sent::Deny, Seen::TurnEnded) => Verdict::Consistent,
        (Sent::Choose(want), Seen::Answered(got)) if want == got => Verdict::Consistent,
        _ => Verdict::Contradicts,
    }
}
```

`mod.rs` adds `pub mod dialogs;` and `pub mod evidence;`.

```rust
async fn answer(r: &Rig, id: &str, rid: &str, option: &str) -> CommandResult {
    r.core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
        id: id.into(), request_id: rid.into(), revision: None, option_id: Some(option.into()), text: None,
    }).await
}

async fn resolution(rx: &mut tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver<ServerMessage>, rid: &str) -> ResolvedBy {
    loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request_id == rid
        {
            return by;
        }
    }
}

fn pending_id(r: &Rig, agent: &str) -> String {
    r.agent(agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).expect("pending request")
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `deliver`**

In `hooks/mod.rs`, replace the Task 10 stub:

```rust
    /// Writes the reply into the request's hold. Nothing reached Claude if
    /// the hold is gone (closed, expired or released): `NotDelivered`, and
    /// the core puts the request back to `open`.
    async fn deliver(&self, request: &Request, answer: &Answer) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        use acd_protocol::Effect;
        let Answer::Option(option_id) = answer else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::NotSupported));
        };
        let Some(effect) = request.options.iter().find(|o| &o.id == option_id).map(|o| o.effect) else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::BadRequest));
        };
        let mut held = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held");
        let Some(h) = held.get_mut(&request.id) else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        };
        let body = match (effect, &h.rule) {
            (Effect::Allow, _) => wire::reply_allow(),
            (Effect::Deny, _) => wire::reply_deny(),
            (Effect::AllowAlways, Some(rule)) => wire::reply_allow_always(rule),
            _ => return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::BadRequest)),
        };
        let Some(reply) = h.reply.take() else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        };
        h.delivered = Some((effect, tokio::time::Instant::now()));
        if reply.send(body).is_err() {
            h.delivered = None;
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
```

```rust
use acd_core::CorrelationKey;
use acd_protocol::{AnswerPath, RequestKind, ResolvedBy};
use super::dialogs::DialogMatch;
use super::evidence::{self, Expect, Verdict};
use super::mapping;
use super::wire::{Payload, always_rule, claude_key, command_of, tool_digest};

#[allow(dead_code)] // `HoldEnded` and `Keys` are constructed from Task 11c
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Phase {
    /// Hook path, hold open.
    Held,
    /// Hook path, hold closed with no delivery; waiting for evidence.
    HoldEnded { since: Instant },
    /// Answer path `keys`: the herdr engine and target watch own it.
    Keys,
    /// Not answerable; ended by evidence or the dialog leaving the screen.
    ReadOnly,
}

#[allow(dead_code)] // `matcher`, `pane`, `capture`, `absent`, `next_check` are read from Task 11c
struct Track {
    agent_id: String,
    session_id: String,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    payload: Payload,
    expect: Expect,
    matcher: DialogMatch,
    pane: Option<PaneFact>,
    phase: Phase,
    opened_at: Instant,
    /// The detection snapshot that matched this request's dialog.
    capture: Option<String>,
    absent: u8,
    next_check: Instant,
}
```

The new methods:

```rust
    fn fallback_key(&self, agent_id: &str) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
        self.core
            .pending_key(agent_id)
            .filter(|(_, _, kind)| *kind == RequestKind::BlockedUnparsed)
            .map(|(_, key, _)| key)
    }

    /// Observes under the per-agent delivery lock for herdr agents (D7).
    async fn observe_locked(&self, agent_id: &str, herdr_agent: bool, obs: Observation) {
        let locks = self.herdr.as_ref().filter(|_| herdr_agent).map(|h| h.agent_locks());
        let _g = match &locks {
            Some(l) => Some(l.lock(agent_id).await),
            None => None,
        };
        self.core.observe(obs);
    }

    fn bound_pane(&self, sid: &str) -> Option<PaneFact> {
        let Owner::Herdr(agent) = &self.sessions.get(sid)?.owner else { return None };
        self.panes().into_iter().find(|p| p.agent_id == *agent)
    }

    async fn open_claude_permission(&mut self, event: HookEvent, hold: Option<(HoldId, oneshot::Sender<String>)>) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let Some(agent_id) = self.sessions.get(&sid).and_then(|s| s.agent_id.clone()) else { return };
        let pane = self.bound_pane(&sid);
        let Some(key) = claude_key(&event.payload) else { return };
        let mut obs = mapping::claude_permission(&agent_id, key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &event.payload);
        if hold.is_none() {
            // Over the hold cap: no hook path. A bound pane can still be
            // answered with keys once its dialog is captured.
            if pane.is_none() {
                return;
            }
            obs.answer_path = AnswerPath::Keys;
            obs.options = mapping::claude_keys_options();
            obs.ready = false;
        }
        self.observe_locked(&agent_id, pane.is_some(), Observation::RequestSeen(obs)).await;
        let Some(rid) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key) else { return };
        if self.tracks.contains_key(&rid) {
            return; // the same occurrence again; the new hold replies empty
        }
        let tool_name = event.payload.tool_name.clone().unwrap_or_default();
        let needle = command_of(&event.payload.tool_input)
            .map(str::to_owned)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| mapping::detail_of(&event.payload.tool_input));
        let phase = match hold {
            Some((hold_id, reply)) => {
                self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").insert(rid.clone(), HeldReq {
                    hold: hold_id,
                    reply: Some(reply),
                    rule: always_rule(&event.payload.permission_suggestions),
                    delivered: None,
                });
                self.holds.insert(hold_id, rid.clone());
                Phase::Held
            }
            None => Phase::ReadOnly, // becomes Keys once captured
        };
        let now = Instant::now();
        self.tracks.insert(rid.clone(), Track {
            agent_id,
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key,
            expect: Expect::ClaudeTool {
                session_id: sid,
                digest: tool_digest(&tool_name, &event.payload.tool_input),
                tool_name,
            },
            matcher: DialogMatch::ClaudePermission { needle },
            payload: event.payload,
            pane: pane.clone(),
            phase,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
        });
        self.sweep();
    }


    fn delivered(&self, rid: &str) -> Option<(Effect, Instant)> {
        self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get(rid).and_then(|h| h.delivered)
    }

    /// Ends a request and forgets it; dropping its reply sender releases a
    /// still-open hold with an empty reply.
    fn end(&mut self, rid: &str, by: ResolvedBy) {
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestEndedById { request_id: rid.to_owned(), by });
        self.forget(rid);
    }

    /// Forgets a request. Its occurrence counts as confirmed gone whatever
    /// the outcome (spec §5.1: the hook source reported the end), so a
    /// repeat of the same reusable key opens a new request (D2).
    fn forget(&mut self, rid: &str) {
        if let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").remove(rid) {
            self.holds.remove(&h.hold);
        }
        self.core.observe(Observation::DialogGoneById { request_id: rid.to_owned() });
        self.tracks.remove(rid);
    }

    /// Drops tracks whose request the registry already resolved
    /// (superseded, agent gone, migrated away with a conflict).
    fn sweep(&mut self) {
        let dead: Vec<String> = self.tracks.keys().filter(|rid| self.core.request(rid).is_none()).cloned().collect();
        for rid in dead {
            if self.delivered(&rid).is_some() {
                tracing::debug!(request_id = %rid, "request ended before its evidence arrived (D10)");
            }
            self.forget(&rid);
        }
    }

    /// Spec §5.1 and §6.2 step 3 for requests the hook path owns.
    fn apply_evidence(&mut self, ev: &HookEvent) {
        let ids: Vec<String> = self
            .tracks
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, t)| t.session_id == ev.payload.session_id && t.phase != Phase::Keys && ev.at >= t.opened_at)
            .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
            .collect();
        for rid in ids {
            let Some(seen) = self.tracks.get(&rid).and_then(|t| evidence::observe(&t.expect, ev)) else { continue };
            let by = match self.delivered(&rid) {
                Some((_, at)) if ev.at < at => continue,
                Some((effect, _)) => match evidence::sent_for_effect(effect).map(|s| evidence::verdict(&s, &seen)) {
                    Some(Verdict::Consistent) => ResolvedBy::Inbox,
                    _ => ResolvedBy::Terminal,
                },
                // The dialog ended without any acd delivery.
                None => ResolvedBy::Terminal,
            };
            self.end(&rid, by);
        }
    }

```

T3 takeover and agent removal reach holds through `sweep` (the request
is gone from the registry, so its reply sender drops). Call
`self.sweep()` at the end of `apply_owner` too, so a hold is released in
the same turn as the takeover.

```rust
    /// Reads `pane`'s detection screen in a spawned task and posts the
    /// result back. `Capture` polls until the matcher finds the dialog or
    /// `capture_window` passes; the others read once.
    fn spawn_read(&self, rid: &str, pane: &PaneFact, purpose: Purpose) {
        let Some(herdr) = &self.herdr else { return };
        let Some(pref) = herdr.panes().read().expect("pane map").get(&pane.agent_id).cloned() else { return };
        let Some(matcher) = self.tracks.get(rid).map(|t| t.matcher.clone()) else { return };
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        let request_id = rid.to_owned();
        let (window, poll) = (self.cfg.capture_window, self.cfg.capture_poll);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let deadline = Instant::now() + window;
            loop {
                let text = pref
                    .herdr
                    .read_screen(&pref.pane_id, "detection", None, Instant::now() + CALL_TIMEOUT)
                    .await
                    .ok()
                    .filter(|r| !r.truncated)
                    .map(|r| r.text);
                let done = purpose != Purpose::Capture
                    || text.as_deref().is_some_and(|t| matcher.find(t).is_some())
                    || Instant::now() >= deadline;
                if done {
                    let text = if purpose == Purpose::Capture {
                        text.filter(|t| matcher.find(t).is_some())
                    } else {
                        text
                    };
                    let _ = tx.send(Internal::Screen { request_id, purpose, text });
                    return;
                }
                tokio::time::sleep(poll).await;
            }
        });
    }


    async fn on_hold_ended(&mut self, hold: HoldId, expired: bool) {
        let Some(rid) = self.holds.remove(&hold) else { return };
        if let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get_mut(&rid) {
            h.reply = None; // a later deliver gets RequestGone
        }
        if self.delivered(&rid).is_some() {
            return; // evidence decides
        }
        let now = Instant::now();
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) else { return };
        let bound = t.pane.clone().filter(|_| t.capture.is_some());
        match bound {
            Some(p) => {
                t.phase = Phase::HoldEnded { since: now };
                self.spawn_read(&rid, &p, Purpose::HoldEnded { expired });
            }
            // Hooks-only, or bound without a captured dialog (spec §6.2 step 6).
            None if expired => self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Lost),
            None => t.phase = Phase::HoldEnded { since: now },
        }
    }

    async fn on_screen(&mut self, rid: String, purpose: Purpose, text: Option<String>) {
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) else { return };
        let matched = text.clone().filter(|s| t.matcher.find(s).is_some());
        match purpose {
            Purpose::Capture => {
                t.capture = matched.clone();
                let unheld = t.phase == Phase::ReadOnly;
                if let (true, Some(screen)) = (unheld, matched) {
                    self.switch_to_keys(&rid, screen).await; // over the hold cap
                }
            }
            Purpose::Presence => {
                if text.is_none() {
                    return; // a failed read is neither present nor absent
                }
                t.absent = if matched.is_some() { 0 } else { t.absent + 1 };
                let gone = t.absent >= 2;
                if gone && self.delivered(&rid).is_none() {
                    self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
                }
            }
            // Closed or expired with no delivery: keys if the dialog is
            // still up, otherwise it ended without acd.
            Purpose::HoldEnded { .. } => match matched {
                Some(screen) => self.switch_to_keys(&rid, screen).await,
                None => self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Terminal),
            },
        }
    }

    /// Spec §6.2 steps 4 and 5 for a herdr-bound request whose hold ended
    /// while its dialog is still up: rebuild it for keys (D9), register a
    /// target from this screen, and only then mark it ready.
    async fn switch_to_keys(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: String) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let Some(dialog) = find_dialog(&screen) else { return };
        let mut obs = mapping::claude_permission(&t.agent_id, t.key.clone(), None, &t.payload);
        obs.answer_path = AnswerPath::Keys;
        obs.options = mapping::claude_keys_options();
        obs.ready = false;
        let target = KeysTarget::from_screen(
            rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &screen, mapping::claude_permission_keys(&dialog),
        );
        let locks = herdr.agent_locks();
        let _g = locks.lock(&t.agent_id).await;
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
        let open_on_keys = self
            .core
            .request(rid)
            .is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        match target {
            Ok(target) if open_on_keys && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok() => {
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
                t.phase = Phase::Keys;
            }
            _ => {
                // No usable target: show it read-only until it ends.
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestPath {
                    agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(),
                    key: t.key.clone(),
                    answer_path: AnswerPath::None,
                });
                t.phase = Phase::ReadOnly;
            }
        }
    }
```

```rust
    fn target(rid: &str) -> crate::herdr::keys::KeysTarget {
        let dialog = std::fs::read_to_string(format!(
            "{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-permission-bash.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        crate::herdr::keys::KeysTarget::from_screen(
            rid.into(), "a".into(), acd_core::CorrelationKey("k".into()), &dialog,
            [("allow".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("deny".to_string(), "4".to_string())].into()).unwrap()

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn the_hub_uses_only_evidence_after_the_key_and_for_this_request() {
        use crate::herdr::keys::Evidence;
        let hub = EvidenceHub::default();
        hub.expect("r1", bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt"));
        let mut post = ev("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json", HookName::PostToolUse);
        hub.publish(&post); // before the key: ignored
        let sent_at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        assert_eq!(
            tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100), hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at)).await.ok(),
            None, "an event before sent_at is not evidence");
        post.at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        hub.publish(&post); // after the key: in the backlog
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::Consistent);
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "deny", sent_at).await, Verdict::Contradicts);
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r2"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::NoEvidence, "never reused across ids");
        hub.forget("r1");
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::NoEvidence);
    }

    #[tokio::test]

        use crate::herdr::keys::Evidence;
        let hub = std::sync::Arc::new(EvidenceHub::default());
        hub.expect("r1", bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt"));
        let sent_at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        let h2 = hub.clone();
        let waiting = tokio::spawn(async move { h2.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await });
        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
        let mut stop = ev("claude/payload-Stop.json", HookName::Stop);
        stop.at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        hub.publish(&stop);
        assert_eq!(waiting.await.unwrap(), Verdict::Contradicts, "allow, then the turn ended without the tool");
    }
```

```rust
fn herdr_screen(name: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

async fn question_rig() -> (Rig, String) {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")], |_| {}).await;
    let q = herdr_screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt");
    let f = r.fake.as_ref().unwrap();
    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &q);
    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "visible", &q);
    f.on_keys(|f, pane, _| {
        let idle = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-idle-detection.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        let prompt = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-idle-prompt.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        f.set_screen(pane, "detection", &idle);
        f.set_screen(pane, "visible", &prompt);
        f.set_status(pane, "working");
    });
    let agent = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().keys().next().unwrap().clone();
    (r, agent)
}

fn post_answer(label: &str) -> Value {
    let mut p = fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
    p["tool_response"]["answers"]["Pick a color"] = label.into();
    p["tool_input"]["answers"]["Pick a color"] = label.into();
    p
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_single_question_on_a_bound_pane_is_answered_with_its_number_key() {
    let (r, agent) = question_rig().await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let h = bound_headers(&r);
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &fx("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json"), &h).await;
    r.hook("claude", "PermissionRequest", &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-AskUserQuestion.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = until("question", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).filter(|p| p.kind == RequestKind::Question).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::Keys);
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "o1").await.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().keys_sent().last().map(|k| k.1.clone()), Some(vec!["1".to_string()]));
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post_answer("Red"), &h).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid).await, ResolvedBy::Inbox);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_question_answered_otherwise_is_terminal() {
    let (r, agent) = question_rig().await;

    let rid = until("question", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).filter(|p| p.kind == RequestKind::Question).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "o1").await;
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post_answer("Blue"), &h).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid).await, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the hub**

Append to `evidence.rs`:

```rust
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::Mutex;

use async_trait::async_trait;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tokio::time::Instant;

use crate::herdr::keys::{Evidence, KeysTarget};

const RECENT: usize = 256;

/// Expectations by request id, plus the recent and live hook events. The
/// keys engine asks it for a verdict after a key (P1 follow-ups: only
/// evidence for `target.request_id` that arrived after `sent_at`, never a
/// verdict reused across ids, bounded by the engine's `evidence_timeout`).
pub struct EvidenceHub {
    expects: Mutex<HashMap<String, Expect>>,
    recent: Mutex<VecDeque<HookEvent>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<HookEvent>,
}

impl Default for EvidenceHub {
    fn default() -> Self {
        EvidenceHub { expects: Mutex::default(), recent: Mutex::default(), tx: broadcast::channel(RECENT).0 }
    }
}

impl EvidenceHub {
    pub fn expect(&self, request_id: &str, e: Expect) {
        self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").insert(request_id.to_owned(), e);
    }

    pub fn forget(&self, request_id: &str) {
        self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").remove(request_id);
    }

    pub fn publish(&self, ev: &HookEvent) {
        {
            let mut r = self.recent.lock().expect("evidence");
            r.push_back(ev.clone());
            while r.len() > RECENT {
                r.pop_front();
            }
        }
        let _ = self.tx.send(ev.clone());
    }
}

pub fn sent_for_option(expect: &Expect, option_id: &str) -> Option<Sent> {
    match expect {
        Expect::ClaudeQuestion { labels, .. } => labels.get(option_id).cloned().map(Sent::Choose),
        _ => match option_id {
            "allow" | "allow_always" => Some(Sent::Allow),
            "deny" => Some(Sent::Deny),
            _ => None,
        },
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl Evidence for EvidenceHub {
    async fn outcome(&self, target: &KeysTarget, option_id: &str, sent_at: Instant) -> Verdict {
        let Some(expect) = self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").get(target.request_id()).cloned() else {
            return Verdict::NoEvidence;
        };
        let Some(sent) = sent_for_option(&expect, option_id) else {
            return Verdict::NoEvidence;
        };
        // Subscribe before reading the backlog so nothing falls between.
        let mut rx = self.tx.subscribe();
        let backlog: Vec<HookEvent> =
            self.recent.lock().expect("evidence").iter().filter(|e| e.at >= sent_at).cloned().collect();
        for ev in &backlog {
            if let Some(seen) = observe(&expect, ev) {
                return verdict(&sent, &seen);
            }
        }
        loop {
            match rx.recv().await {
                Ok(ev) if ev.at >= sent_at => {
                    if let Some(seen) = observe(&expect, &ev) {
                        return verdict(&sent, &seen);
                    }
                }
                Ok(_) | Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_)) => {}
                Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed) => return Verdict::NoEvidence,
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
    async fn open_claude_question(&mut self, event: HookEvent) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let Some(agent_id) = self.sessions.get(&sid).and_then(|s| s.agent_id.clone()) else { return };
        let pane = self.bound_pane(&sid);
        let Some(key) = claude_key(&event.payload) else { return };
        let Some(obs) =
            mapping::claude_question(&agent_id, key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &event.payload, pane.is_some())
        else {
            return;
        };
        let answerable = obs.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys;
        self.observe_locked(&agent_id, pane.is_some(), Observation::RequestSeen(obs)).await;
        let Some(rid) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key) else { return };
        if self.tracks.contains_key(&rid) {
            return; // the PreToolUse / PermissionRequest pair (spec §6.2)
        }
        let q0 = &event.payload.tool_input["questions"][0];
        let raw_labels: Vec<String> = q0["options"]
            .as_array()
            .map(|a| a.iter().filter_map(|o| o["label"].as_str().map(str::to_owned)).collect())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let labels = raw_labels.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, l)| (format!("o{}", i + 1), l.clone())).collect();
        let now = Instant::now();
        self.tracks.insert(rid.clone(), Track {
            agent_id,
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key,
            expect: Expect::ClaudeQuestion {
                session_id: sid,
                question: q0["question"].as_str().unwrap_or_default().to_owned(),
                labels,
            },
            matcher: DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { labels: raw_labels },
            kind: TrackKind::Question,
            payload: event.payload,
            pane: pane.clone(),
            phase: Phase::ReadOnly,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
        });
        if let (true, Some(p)) = (answerable, pane) {
            self.spawn_read(&rid, &p, Purpose::Capture);
        }
        self.sweep();
    }

    /// A captured single question: register its number keys, then mark it
    /// ready. Any failure leaves it read-only (spec §6.2 Questions).
    async fn register_question(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: Option<String>) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let Expect::ClaudeQuestion { labels, .. } = &t.expect else { return };
        let target = screen.and_then(|s| {
            KeysTarget::from_screen(rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &s, mapping::question_keys(labels.len())).ok()
        });
        let locks = herdr.agent_locks();
        let _g = locks.lock(&t.agent_id).await;
        let open = self.core.request(rid).is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        match target {
            Some(target) if open && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok() => {
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
                t.phase = Phase::Keys;
                self.evidence.expect(rid, t.expect.clone());
            }
            _ => self.core.observe(Observation::RequestPath {
                agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(),
                key: t.key.clone(),
                answer_path: AnswerPath::None,
            }),
        }
    }
```

In `on_screen`'s `Purpose::Capture` arm, dispatch on the kind:

```rust
            Purpose::Capture => {
                t.capture = matched.clone();
                if matched.is_some()
                    && let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get_mut(&rid)

                    h.bound_captured = true; // Task 11c

                    TrackKind::Permission if phase == Phase::ReadOnly => {

                        }
```

```rust
mod common;
use common::*;

use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::fake::fake_agent;
use acd_protocol::*;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

const CODEX_SID: &str = "01a116d3-e8a3-7ef0-9f57-79c5413ed009";

fn herdr_screen(name: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

/// The recorded payload, re-pointed at the command the recorded dialog shows.
fn permission(cwd: &str) -> Value {
    let mut p = fx("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    p["tool_input"]["command"] = "touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt".into();
    p["cwd"] = cwd.into();
    p
}

fn codex_pane(id: &str, cwd: &str, session: Option<&str>) -> Value {
    let mut a = fake_agent(id, "codex", "working");
    a["cwd"] = cwd.into();
    a["foreground_cwd"] = cwd.into();
    if let Some(s) = session {
        a["agent_session"] = json!({"source": "herdr:codex", "agent": "codex", "kind": "id", "value": s});
    }
    a
}

async fn answer(r: &Rig, id: &str, rid: &str, option: &str) -> CommandResult {
    r.core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
        id: id.into(), request_id: rid.into(), revision: None, option_id: Some(option.into()), text: None,
    }).await
}

fn agent_of(r: &Rig, pane: &str) -> String {
    r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().iter().find(|(_, p)| p.pane_id == pane).unwrap().0.clone()
}

    let cwd = tmp.path().to_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let r = rig(&[codex_pane("w1:p1", &cwd, Some(CODEX_SID))], |_| {}).await;
    let f = r.fake.as_ref().unwrap();
    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-collapsed-small-pane-detection.txt"));
    let agent = agent_of(&r, "w1:p1");
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await;
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    assert!(r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().detail.ends_with("Enlarge the pane to answer here."));
    assert_eq!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await.error_code(), Some(ErrorCode::RequestNotReady));
    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt"));
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    assert!(!r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().detail.contains("Enlarge"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn codex_outcomes_follow_post_tool_use_and_stop() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();

        let prompt = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/codex-idle-prompt.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        f.set_screen(pane, "detection", &idle);

    let agent = agent_of(&r, "w1:p1");

    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await;
```

Also add the reappearing-dialog case (P1 follow-up: a dialog with the
same identity that comes back within about two polls keeps the old
occurrence open, so answers get `screen_mismatch`):

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_repeated_dialog_opens_a_new_occurrence_with_a_fresh_target() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cwd = tmp.path().to_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let r = rig(&[codex_pane("w1:p1", &cwd, Some(CODEX_SID))], |_| {}).await;
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt"));
    let agent = agent_of(&r, "w1:p1");
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await;
    let r1 = until("first", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("first target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&r1)).await;
    // Answered in the terminal and asked again before the watch saw it go.
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await;
    loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request_id == r1
        {
            assert_eq!(by, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
            break;
        }
    }
    let r2 = until("second", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).filter(|id| *id != r1)).await;
    until("second target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&r2)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &r2, "allow").await.is_ok());
}
```

In `state.rs`:

```rust
use super::owner::{CodexBind, codex_bind};
use super::wire::codex_key;

pub(crate) enum CodexSeen {
    /// The detection snapshot that shows this request's command.
    Full(String),
    /// A collapsed Codex dialog (pane too short): it cannot be matched.
    Collapsed,
    Nothing,
}

// `Internal` gains:
//     Codex { event: HookEvent, pane: PaneFact, key: CorrelationKey, command: String, seen: CodexSeen },
```

```rust
    async fn on_codex(&mut self, event: HookEvent) {
        self.apply_evidence(&event);
        if event.name != HookName::PermissionRequest {
            return;
        }
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        if self.t3.as_ref().is_some_and(|m| m.owner(&sid).is_some()) {
            return; // T3 shows it (ownership rule 1 holds for Codex sessions too)
        }
        if event.hints.herdr_pane.is_some() {
            tracing::debug!("Codex HERDR_* hints are not used for binding (gate 4)");
        }
        let (Some(key), Some(command)) = (codex_key(&event.payload), command_of(&event.payload.tool_input).map(str::to_owned)) else {
            return;
        };
        let pane = match codex_bind(&sid, event.payload.cwd.as_deref(), &self.panes()) {
            CodexBind::Session(p) | CodexBind::OnlyInCwd(p) => p,
            CodexBind::Ambiguous | CodexBind::Unbound => {
                tracing::debug!("Codex request not bound; the herdr fallback shows it read-only");
                return;
            }
        };
        self.spawn_codex_watch(event, pane, key, command);
    }

    /// Polls the bound pane for up to `codex_window` (spec §6.3: 1 s).
    fn spawn_codex_watch(&self, event: HookEvent, pane: PaneFact, key: CorrelationKey, command: String) {
        let Some(herdr) = &self.herdr else { return };
        let Some(pref) = herdr.panes().read().expect("pane map").get(&pane.agent_id).cloned() else { return };
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        let (window, poll) = (self.cfg.codex_window, self.cfg.capture_poll);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let matcher = DialogMatch::CodexPermission { needle: command.clone() };
            let deadline = Instant::now() + window;
            let mut collapsed = false;
            let seen = loop {
                if let Ok(r) = pref.herdr.read_screen(&pref.pane_id, "detection", None, Instant::now() + CALL_TIMEOUT).await
                    && !r.truncated
                {
                    if matcher.find(&r.text).is_some() {
                        break CodexSeen::Full(r.text);
                    }
                    collapsed |= DialogMatch::collapsed_codex(&r.text);
                }
                if Instant::now() >= deadline {
                    break if collapsed { CodexSeen::Collapsed } else { CodexSeen::Nothing };
                }
                tokio::time::sleep(poll).await;
            };
            let _ = tx.send(Internal::Codex { event, pane, key, command, seen });
        });
    }

    async fn on_codex_seen(&mut self, event: HookEvent, pane: PaneFact, key: CorrelationKey, command: String, seen: CodexSeen) {
        let collapsed = match &seen {
            CodexSeen::Nothing => return, // misbound or gone: open nothing
            CodexSeen::Collapsed => true,
            CodexSeen::Full(_) => false,
        };
        let agent_id = pane.agent_id.clone();
        let obs = mapping::codex_permission(&agent_id, key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &event.payload, collapsed);
        self.observe_locked(&agent_id, true, Observation::RequestSeen(obs)).await;
        let Some(rid) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key) else { return };
        let now = Instant::now();
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        self.tracks.entry(rid.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Track {
            agent_id: agent_id.clone(),
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key: key.clone(),
            expect: Expect::CodexTool {
                session_id: sid,
                tool_name: event.payload.tool_name.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
                command: command.clone(),
            },
            matcher: DialogMatch::CodexPermission { needle: command },
            kind: TrackKind::Codex,
            payload: event.payload.clone(),
            pane: Some(pane),
            phase: Phase::ReadOnly,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
        });
        if let CodexSeen::Full(screen) = seen {
            self.register_codex(&rid, screen).await;
        }
        self.sweep();
    }

    async fn register_codex(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: String) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let keys = mapping::CODEX_KEYS.iter().map(|(o, k)| (o.to_string(), k.to_string())).collect();
        let target = KeysTarget::from_screen(rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &screen, keys);
        let locks = herdr.agent_locks();
        let _g = locks.lock(&t.agent_id).await;
        // Drop the "Enlarge the pane" note now that the command is visible.
        let obs = mapping::codex_permission(&t.agent_id, t.key.clone(), None, &t.payload, false);
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
        let open = self.core.request(rid).is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        if let Ok(target) = target
            && open
            && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok()
        {
            self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
            t.phase = Phase::Keys;
            t.capture = Some(screen);
            self.evidence.expect(rid, t.expect.clone());
        }
    }
```

A new hook dialog for a key whose previous occurrence is still open
ends that occurrence first, so the registry opens a new request id and
the new dialog gets its own target (D8 forbids replacing a target):

```rust
    /// The hook reports a new dialog while the old occurrence of the same
    /// key is still open: it was answered in the terminal and asked again
    /// before the target watch saw it go.
    fn restart_occurrence(&mut self, rid: &str) {
        if let Some(h) = &self.herdr {
            h.keys_targets().remove(rid);
        }
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestEndedIfOpen { request_id: rid.to_owned(), by: ResolvedBy::Terminal });
        self.core.observe(Observation::DialogGoneById { request_id: rid.to_owned() });
        self.forget(rid);
    }
```

Call it at the top of `on_codex_seen` (before `RequestSeen`), and in
the two Claude open paths, right after computing the key:

```rust
        if let Some(old) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key)
            && self.tracks.get(&old).is_some_and(|t| t.phase != Phase::Held)
        {
            self.restart_occurrence(&old);
        }
```

For Claude questions, only a `PreToolUse` starts a new occurrence; the
`PermissionRequest` that follows it for the same `AskUserQuestion` is
the same dialog (spec §6.2), so `open_claude_question` applies the check
only when `event.name == HookName::PreToolUse`. A `PermissionRequest`
for a tool always means a new dialog. A held old occurrence is left
alone: its own hold, evidence or close decides it.

Wire it up:

- `on_internal`: `Internal::Codex { event, pane, key, command, seen } => self.on_codex_seen(event, pane, key, command, seen).await`.
- `on_screen`'s `Purpose::Capture` arm: `TrackKind::Codex => if let Some(s) = matched { self.register_codex(&rid, s).await }`.
- `on_tick`: a Codex track in `Phase::ReadOnly` with no capture is
  re-read until its full dialog shows:

```rust
                (Phase::ReadOnly, None) if t.kind == TrackKind::Codex && t.capture.is_none() && now >= t.next_check => {
                    t.next_check = now + self.cfg.capture_window + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll;
                    if let Some(p) = &t.pane {
                        captures.push((rid.clone(), p.clone()));
                    }
                }
```

  with `let mut captures = Vec::new();` declared next to `reads`, and
  after the loop `for (rid, p) in captures { self.spawn_read(&rid, &p, Purpose::Capture); }`.
  Put this arm before the `Held | ReadOnly` presence arm.

A Codex request that is answered in the terminal ends through the herdr
target watch (dialog gone, `terminal`) once it has a target, and through
`apply_evidence` (`PostToolUse` or `Stop`, `terminal`) while it is still
collapsed.

### Task 14: Secrets hardening and `init` repair

P1 follow-ups: `check_mode` must not follow symlinks and must check the
owner; `write_secret` must clean up its temp file on failure and fsync
the directory after the rename; `init` must check the mode of files it
keeps and reject an empty `machine-id`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-securefs/src/lib.rs` (`check_private_file`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/secrets.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/identity.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`init`)
- Test: `crates/acd/src/secrets.rs` unit tests, `crates/acd/tests/cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `acd_securefs::check_private_file(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<(), SecureFsError>`.
  - `secrets::check_mode(path, max_mode)`: same signature, now no-follow
    and owner-checked; the "Fix with: chmod" hint is kept for mode errors.
  - `secrets::read_secret(path)`: reads through `read_private` (0600, 4 KiB).
  - `secrets::write_secret(path, value)`: unique temp name ending in
    `.tmp`, removed on any failure, parent directory fsynced.
  - `secrets::repair_mode(path: &Path, mode: u32) -> Result<bool>`: for
    `init`; refuses symlinks and foreign owners, tightens a loose mode and
    returns whether it changed.
  - `identity::machine_id(dir)` errors on an empty id.

```rust
fn init_repairs_loose_modes_of_files_it_keeps() {
```

```rust
/// No-follow open, regular file, owned by the euid, mode within `max_mode`.
pub fn check_private_file(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<(), SecureFsError> {
    let file = open_path(path, false)?;
    verify(&file, path, false, max_mode)
}
```

`secrets.rs`:

```rust
use acd_securefs::SecureFsError;

fn explain(e: SecureFsError) -> anyhow::Error {
    match e {
        SecureFsError::Mode { path, mode, want } => anyhow::anyhow!(
            "{} has mode {:o}, which lets other users read it. Fix with: chmod {:o} {}",
            path.display(), mode & 0o777, want, path.display()
        ),
        SecureFsError::Symlink(p) => anyhow::anyhow!("{} is a symlink; replace it with a regular file", p.display()),
        SecureFsError::Owner(p) => anyhow::anyhow!("{} is not owned by you", p.display()),
        other => anyhow::anyhow!("{other}"),
    }
}

/// Fails if `path` is a symlink, is not owned by the effective user, or
/// grants any permission bit outside `max_mode`.
pub fn check_mode(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<()> {
    let meta = fs::symlink_metadata(path).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
    let r = if meta.is_dir() {
        acd_securefs::check_private_dir(path, max_mode)
    } else {
        acd_securefs::check_private_file(path, max_mode)
    };
    r.map_err(explain)
}

pub fn read_secret(path: &Path) -> Result<String> {
    let bytes = acd_securefs::read_private(path, 0o600, 4096).map_err(explain)?;
    Ok(String::from_utf8(bytes).context("secret is not UTF-8")?.trim().to_string())
}

/// Writes `value` atomically with mode 0600: a unique temp file in the
/// same directory, fsync, rename, fsync of the directory. The temp file is
/// removed on any failure.
pub fn write_secret(path: &Path, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
    let dir = path.parent().context("secret path has no parent")?;
    let mut rnd = [0u8; 8];
    getrandom::fill(&mut rnd).expect("OS randomness is available");
    let name = path.file_name().context("secret path has no file name")?.to_string_lossy();
    let tmp = dir.join(format!(
        "{name}.{}.{}.tmp",
        std::process::id(),
        rnd.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect::<String>()
    ));
    let result = (|| -> Result<()> {
        let mut f = OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).mode(0o600).open(&tmp)
            .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", tmp.display()))?;
        f.write_all(value.as_bytes())?;
        f.write_all(b"\n")?;
        f.sync_all()?;
        fs::rename(&tmp, path).with_context(|| format!("replacing {}", path.display()))?;
        fs::File::open(dir)?.sync_all()?;
        Ok(())
    })();
    if result.is_err() {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp);
    }
    result
}

/// For `acd init`: tightens a loose mode on a regular file the user owns.
/// Refuses symlinks and foreign owners. Returns whether it changed.
pub fn repair_mode(path: &Path, mode: u32) -> Result<bool> {
    use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;
    let meta = fs::symlink_metadata(path).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
    if meta.file_type().is_symlink() {
        bail!("{} is a symlink; replace it with a regular file", path.display());
    }
    if !meta.is_file() {
        bail!("{} is not a regular file", path.display());
    }
    if meta.uid() != acd_securefs::euid() {
        bail!("{} is not owned by you", path.display());
    }
    if meta.mode() & 0o777 & !mode == 0 {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    fs::set_permissions(path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(mode))?;
    Ok(true)
}
```

```rust
    let id = id.trim();
```

```rust
    let id_path = dir.join("machine-id");
    let id = match identity::machine_id(dir) {
        Ok(id) => id,
        Err(_) if !id_path.exists() || std::fs::read_to_string(&id_path).is_ok_and(|s| s.trim().is_empty()) => {
            created.push("machine-id");
            identity::create_machine_id(dir)?
        }
        Err(e) => return Err(e),
    };
```

and print `repaired: …` when the list is not empty. Note that
`repair_mode` runs before the token loop, so a symlinked token makes
`init` fail without touching anything.

### Task 15: `acd hook codex <event>`: the Codex shim

Spec §6.1: reads stdin up to 1 MiB, forwards it with the hook token,
prints acd's body and exits 0; any failure prints nothing; connect
timeout 500 ms.

- Produces:

`crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`:

```rust
mod common;
use common::*;

use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::TcpListener;
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::sync::mpsc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// A one-shot HTTP server: records the request, replies with `status` and
/// `body`, or never replies when `stall`.
fn one_shot(status: u16, body: &'static str, stall: bool) -> (u16, mpsc::Receiver<String>) {
    let l = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let port = l.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let Ok((mut s, _)) = l.accept() else { return };
        s.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2))).unwrap();
        let mut req = Vec::new();
        let mut buf = [0u8; 65536];
        loop {
            match s.read(&mut buf) {
                Ok(0) | Err(_) => break,
                Ok(n) => {
                    req.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n]);
                    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&req);
                    if let Some((head, rest)) = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n") {
                        let len = head.lines().find_map(|l| l.to_ascii_lowercase().strip_prefix("content-length: ").map(|v| v.trim().parse::<usize>().unwrap())).unwrap_or(0);
                        if rest.len() >= len { break; }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        let _ = tx.send(String::from_utf8_lossy(&req).into_owned());
        if stall {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5));
            return;
        }
        let _ = write!(s, "HTTP/1.1 {status} X\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len());
    });
    (port, rx)
}

fn shim_dir(port: u16) -> (tempfile::TempDir, std::path::PathBuf) {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let dir = init_dir(tmp.path());
    std::fs::write(dir.join("config.toml"), format!("hook_port = {port}\n")).unwrap();
    (tmp, dir)
}

fn run_shim(dir: &std::path::Path, event: &str, stdin: &[u8], env: &[(&str, &str)]) -> (std::process::Output, Duration) {
    let mut cmd = acd(dir);
    for (k, v) in env { cmd.env(k, v); }
    let mut child = cmd.args(["hook", "codex", event, "acd-managed:v1"])
        .stdin(Stdio::piped()).stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(Stdio::piped()).spawn().unwrap();
    let t = Instant::now();
    child.stdin.take().unwrap().write_all(stdin).ok();
    let out = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
    (out, t.elapsed())
}

fn codex_fixture(name: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    std::fs::read(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/codex/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_forwards_the_hook_and_prints_the_reply() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "{\"ok\":1}", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let token = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook.token")).unwrap().trim().to_string();
    let body = codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &body, &[("HERDR_PANE_ID", "w1:p2"), ("HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "")]);
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout), "{\"ok\":1}");
    let req = rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap();
    assert!(req.starts_with("POST /hook/codex/PermissionRequest HTTP/1.1\r\n"), "{req}");
    assert!(req.contains(&format!("X-Acd-Token: {token}\r\n")));
    assert!(req.contains("X-Herdr-Pane: w1:p2\r\n"));
    assert!(!req.contains("X-Herdr-Socket"), "empty env values are not sent");
    assert!(req.ends_with(std::str::from_utf8(&body).unwrap()));
    assert!(!String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).contains(&token) && !String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains(&token));
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_fails_open_when_acd_is_down() {
    let port = free_port(); // nothing listens
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, took) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert!(out.stdout.is_empty() && out.stderr.is_empty());
    assert!(took < Duration::from_secs(1), "{took:?}");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_fails_open_when_acd_stalls() {
    let (port, _rx) = one_shot(200, "", true);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, took) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
    assert!(took < Duration::from_millis(1200), "inside Codex's 1 s budget plus process start: {took:?}");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_prints_nothing_for_a_non_2xx_reply() {
    let (port, _rx) = one_shot(401, "{\"no\":1}", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", b"{\"session_id\":\"s\"}", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_refuses_oversized_input_without_sending() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "x", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let big = vec![b'a'; (1 << 20) + 1];
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", &big, &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(300)).is_err(), "nothing was sent");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_without_a_token_prints_nothing() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "x", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    std::fs::remove_file(dir.join("hook.token")).unwrap();
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", b"{}", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty() && out.stderr.is_empty());
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(300)).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_honours_an_explicit_config_dir() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "ok", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let other_home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mut cmd = acd_home(other_home.path(), other_home.path());
    cmd.env_remove("ACD_CONFIG_DIR");
    let out = cmd.args(["hook", "codex", "Stop", "--config-dir"]).arg(&dir).arg("acd-managed:v1")
        .stdin(Stdio::null()).output().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout), "ok");
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(1)).is_ok());
}
```

`hook_shim.rs`:

```rust
//! `acd hook codex <event>` (spec §6.1): Codex's command hook. Reads the
//! payload on stdin, forwards it to the local hook endpoint, prints the
//! reply body and exits 0. Any failure prints nothing and still exits 0, so
//! Codex shows its own dialog. It never logs and never prints the token.
use std::ffi::OsString;
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::{SocketAddr, TcpStream};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

pub const MAX_BODY: usize = 1 << 20;
const CONNECT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_millis(500);

pub fn main(args: &[OsString]) -> i32 {
    if let Some(body) = run(args) {
        let mut out = std::io::stdout().lock();
        let _ = out.write_all(body.as_bytes());
        let _ = out.flush();
    }
    0
}

/// Inside Codex's own timeouts (§6.3: 1 s for PermissionRequest, 2 s else).
fn budget(event: &str) -> Duration {
    if event == "PermissionRequest" { Duration::from_millis(800) } else { Duration::from_millis(1500) }
}

fn header_safe(v: &str) -> bool {
    !v.is_empty() && v.len() <= 1024 && !v.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

fn run(args: &[OsString]) -> Option<String> {
    let mut it = args.iter().map(|a| a.to_str());
    let agent = it.next()??;
    let event = it.next()??;
    if agent != "codex" || event.is_empty() || event.len() > 64 || !event.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
        return None;
    }
    let mut config_dir: Option<PathBuf> = None;
    while let Some(a) = it.next() {
        match a? {
            "--config-dir" => config_dir = Some(PathBuf::from(it.next()??)),
            "acd-managed:v1" => {}
            _ => return None,
        }
    }
    let deadline = Instant::now() + budget(event);
    let mut body = Vec::new();
    std::io::stdin().lock().take(MAX_BODY as u64 + 1).read_to_end(&mut body).ok()?;
    if body.len() > MAX_BODY {
        return None;
    }
    let dir = match config_dir {
        Some(d) => d,
        None => crate::paths::config_dir().ok()?,
    };
    let token = crate::secrets::read_secret(&dir.join("hook.token")).ok()?;
    let port = crate::config::Config::load(&dir).ok()?.hook_port;
    let mut headers = vec![("X-Acd-Token".to_string(), token)];
    // Hints only (D17): acd never binds Codex with them.
    for (env, header) in [("HERDR_PANE_ID", "X-Herdr-Pane"), ("HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "X-Herdr-Socket")] {
        if let Ok(v) = std::env::var(env)
            && header_safe(&v)
        {
            headers.push((header.to_string(), v));
        }
    }
    post(port, &format!("/hook/codex/{event}"), &headers, &body, deadline)
}

fn post(port: u16, path: &str, headers: &[(String, String)], body: &[u8], deadline: Instant) -> Option<String> {
    let addr = SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], port));
    let mut s = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&addr, CONNECT_TIMEOUT).ok()?;
    let left = deadline.checked_duration_since(Instant::now())?;
    s.set_write_timeout(Some(left)).ok()?;
    let mut req = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    for (k, v) in headers {
        req.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\r\n"));
    }
    req.push_str("\r\n");
    s.write_all(req.as_bytes()).ok()?;
    s.write_all(body).ok()?;
    let mut resp = Vec::new();
    let mut buf = [0u8; 8192];
    loop {
        let left = deadline.checked_duration_since(Instant::now())?;
        s.set_read_timeout(Some(left)).ok()?;
        match s.read(&mut buf) {
            Ok(0) => break,
            Ok(n) => {
                resp.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n]);
                if resp.len() > MAX_BODY + 8192 {
                    return None;
                }
            }
            Err(_) => return None,
        }
    }
    parse_response(&resp)
}

/// The body of a 2xx reply with a plain (non-chunked) body.
fn parse_response(resp: &[u8]) -> Option<String> {
    let text = std::str::from_utf8(resp).ok()?;
    let (head, body) = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n")?;
    let status: u16 = head.split_whitespace().nth(1)?.parse().ok()?;
    if !(200..300).contains(&status) {
        return None;
    }
    if head.lines().any(|l| l.to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("transfer-encoding:")) {
        return None;
    }
    Some(body.to_owned())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn only_plain_2xx_bodies_are_printed() {
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n{}").as_deref(), Some("{}"));
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\ncontent-length: 0\r\n\r\n").as_deref(), Some(""));
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\n\r\nno"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n\r\n2\r\n{}\r\n0\r\n\r\n"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"garbage"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unexpected_arguments_send_nothing() {
        let a = |v: &[&str]| v.iter().map(OsString::from).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert!(run(&a(&["claude", "Stop"])).is_none());
        assert!(run(&a(&["codex", "Stop;rm"])).is_none());
        assert!(run(&a(&["codex", "Stop", "--surprise"])).is_none());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the JSON layer**

`crates/acd/Cargo.toml`: `serde_json = { workspace = true, features = ["raw_value"] }`.

`hooks_install/json.rs`:

```rust
//! Order-preserving edits of a JSON object (D16): only the keys acd edits
//! are re-serialized; every other value keeps its original bytes.
use serde::de::{Deserializer, MapAccess, Visitor};
use serde_json::value::RawValue;

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Obj(pub Vec<(String, Box<RawValue>)>);

impl<'de> serde::Deserialize<'de> for Obj {
    fn deserialize<D: Deserializer<'de>>(d: D) -> Result<Obj, D::Error> {
        struct V;
        impl<'de> Visitor<'de> for V {
            type Value = Obj;
            fn expecting(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter) -> std::fmt::Result {
                f.write_str("a JSON object")
            }
            fn visit_map<A: MapAccess<'de>>(self, mut m: A) -> Result<Obj, A::Error> {
                let mut out = Vec::new();
                while let Some((k, v)) = m.next_entry::<String, Box<RawValue>>()? {
                    out.push((k, v));
                }
                Ok(Obj(out))
            }
        }
        d.deserialize_map(V)
    }
}

impl Obj {
    pub fn parse(text: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Obj> {
        if text.trim().is_empty() {
            return Ok(Obj::default());
        }
        Ok(serde_json::from_str(text)?)
    }

    pub fn get(&self, k: &str) -> Option<&RawValue> {
        self.0.iter().find(|(key, _)| key == k).map(|(_, v)| v.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn set(&mut self, k: &str, v: Box<RawValue>) {
        match self.0.iter_mut().find(|(key, _)| key == k) {
            Some(e) => e.1 = v,
            None => self.0.push((k.to_owned(), v)),
        }
    }

    pub fn remove(&mut self, k: &str) {
        self.0.retain(|(key, _)| key != k)
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.is_empty()
    }

    /// Two-space indent at this level; nested values keep their bytes.
    pub fn render(&self) -> String {
        if self.0.is_empty() {
            return "{}".into();
        }
        let mut s = String::from("{\n");
        for (i, (k, v)) in self.0.iter().enumerate() {
            s.push_str("  ");
            s.push_str(&serde_json::to_string(k).expect("a string serializes"));
            s.push_str(": ");
            s.push_str(v.get());
            s.push_str(if i + 1 < self.0.len() { ",\n" } else { "\n" });
        }
        s.push('}');
        s
    }
}

pub fn raw(v: &serde_json::Value) -> Box<RawValue> {
    RawValue::from_string(serde_json::to_string_pretty(v).expect("json")).expect("valid json")
}

pub fn raw_text(s: String) -> anyhow::Result<Box<RawValue>> {
    Ok(RawValue::from_string(s)?)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the entries**

`hooks_install/entries.rs`:

```rust
//! The hook entries acd owns (spec §6.2, §6.3 tables). Every entry carries
//! `acd-managed:v1`; ownership is matched by that marker only.
use serde_json::{Value, json};

use super::{Ctx, MARKER};

/// Claude: `(event, matcher, timeout seconds)`. No SessionStart: Claude
/// never sends it over http (gate 2 draft).
pub fn claude_events(permission_hold: u64) -> Vec<(&'static str, Option<&'static str>, u64)> {
    vec![
        ("UserPromptSubmit", None, 1),
        ("PermissionRequest", None, permission_hold + 10),
        ("PreToolUse", Some("AskUserQuestion"), 1),
        ("PostToolUse", None, 1),
        ("Stop", None, 1),
        ("SessionEnd", None, 1),
    ]
}

pub const CODEX_EVENTS: [(&str, u64); 5] =
    [("SessionStart", 2), ("UserPromptSubmit", 2), ("PermissionRequest", 1), ("PostToolUse", 2), ("Stop", 2)];

pub fn claude_group(ctx: &Ctx, token: &str, event: &str, matcher: Option<&str>, timeout: u64) -> Value {
    let entry = json!({
        "type": "http",
        "url": format!("http://127.0.0.1:{}/hook/claude/{event}?marker={MARKER}", ctx.hook_port),
        "timeout": timeout,
        "headers": {
            "X-Acd-Token": token,
            "X-Herdr-Pane": "$HERDR_PANE_ID",
            "X-Herdr-Socket": "$HERDR_SOCKET_PATH",
            "X-Claude-Entrypoint": "$CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"
        },
        "allowedEnvVars": ["HERDR_PANE_ID", "HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"]
    });
    match matcher {
        Some(m) => json!({"matcher": m, "hooks": [entry]}),
        None => json!({"hooks": [entry]}),
    }
}

/// POSIX single-quote quoting.
pub fn sh_quote(s: &str) -> String {
    format!("'{}'", s.replace('\'', "'\\''"))
}

pub fn codex_group(ctx: &Ctx, event: &str, timeout: u64) -> Value {
    let command = format!(
        "{} hook codex {event} --config-dir {} {MARKER}",
        sh_quote(&ctx.exe.to_string_lossy()),
        sh_quote(&ctx.config_dir.to_string_lossy())
    );
    json!({"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command, "timeout": timeout}]})
}

pub fn is_owned_entry(e: &Value) -> bool {
    ["url", "command"].iter().any(|k| e.get(k).and_then(Value::as_str).is_some_and(|s| s.contains(MARKER)))
}
```

```rust
//! `acd hooks install|uninstall claude|codex` (spec §6.1 Installation).
// Task 16b uses everything here; until then nothing calls it.
#![allow(dead_code)]
mod entries;
mod json;

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::{Context, Result};

pub const MARKER: &str = "acd-managed:v1";

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, clap::ValueEnum)]
pub enum Target {
    Claude,
    Codex,
}

pub struct Ctx {
    pub home: PathBuf,
    pub claude_dir: PathBuf,
    pub codex_dir: PathBuf,
    pub config_dir: PathBuf,
    pub exe: PathBuf,
    pub hook_port: u16,
    pub permission_hold: u64,
}

impl Ctx {
    pub fn from_env(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<Ctx> {
        let home = PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os("HOME").context("HOME is not set")?);
        let cfg = crate::config::Config::load(config_dir)?;
        let env_dir = |k: &str| std::env::var_os(k).filter(|v| !v.is_empty()).map(PathBuf::from);
        Ok(Ctx {
            claude_dir: env_dir("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR").unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".claude")),
            codex_dir: env_dir("CODEX_HOME").unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".codex")),
            home,
            config_dir: std::fs::canonicalize(config_dir)?,
            exe: std::fs::canonicalize(std::env::current_exe()?)?,
            hook_port: cfg.hook_port,
            permission_hold: cfg.claude.permission_hold,
        })
    }

    pub fn settings_path(&self, t: Target) -> PathBuf {
        match t {
            Target::Claude => self.claude_dir.join("settings.json"),
            Target::Codex => self.codex_dir.join("hooks.json"),
        }
    }
}
```
