```rust
fn home_with_acd() -> (tempfile::TempDir, std::path::PathBuf) {
    let home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let dir = init_dir(home.path());
    (home, dir)
}

fn install(dir: &std::path::Path, home: &std::path::Path, target: &str, extra: &[&str]) -> std::process::Output {
    acd_home(dir, home).args(["hooks", "install", target]).args(extra).output().unwrap()
}

fn uninstall(dir: &std::path::Path, home: &std::path::Path, target: &str) -> std::process::Output {
    acd_home(dir, home).args(["hooks", "uninstall", target]).output().unwrap()
}

fn json_of(p: &std::path::Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

fn owned_count(v: &serde_json::Value) -> usize {
    v["hooks"].as_object().map_or(0, |events| events.values()
        .flat_map(|groups| groups.as_array().into_iter().flatten())
        .flat_map(|g| g["hooks"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
        .filter(|e| e.to_string().contains("acd-managed:v1"))
        .count())
}

const FOREIGN_CLAUDE: &str = r#"{
 "permissions": {"allow": ["Bash(npm test)"]},
 "hooks": {
  "Stop": [{"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "say done"}]}],
  "PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Bash", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "guard.sh"}]}]
 },
 "model": "opus"
}"#;

#[test]
fn claude_install_writes_owned_entries_with_the_token_at_mode_0600() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    std::fs::write(dir.join("config.toml"), "hook_port = 47999\n").unwrap();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    let settings = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    assert_eq!(mode(&settings), 0o600);
    let v = json_of(&settings);
    let token = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook.token")).unwrap().trim().to_string();
    for (event, timeout) in [("UserPromptSubmit", 1), ("PermissionRequest", 3610), ("PreToolUse", 1), ("PostToolUse", 1), ("Stop", 1), ("SessionEnd", 1)] {
        let e = &v["hooks"][event][0]["hooks"][0];
        assert_eq!(e["type"], "http", "{event}");
        assert_eq!(e["timeout"], timeout, "{event}");
        assert_eq!(e["url"], format!("http://127.0.0.1:47999/hook/claude/{event}?marker=acd-managed:v1"));
        assert_eq!(e["headers"]["X-Acd-Token"], token.as_str());
        assert_eq!(e["headers"]["X-Herdr-Pane"], "$HERDR_PANE_ID");
        assert_eq!(e["allowedEnvVars"], serde_json::json!(["HERDR_PANE_ID", "HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"]));
    }
    assert_eq!(v["hooks"]["PreToolUse"][0]["matcher"], "AskUserQuestion");
    assert!(v["hooks"].get("SessionStart").is_none(), "Claude sends no http SessionStart (gate 2)");
    assert_eq!(mode(&dir.join("hooks-installed.json")), 0o600);
    for o in [&out.stdout, &out.stderr] {
        assert!(!String::from_utf8_lossy(o).contains(&token), "the token is never printed");
    }
}

fn mode(p: &std::path::Path) -> u32 {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    std::fs::metadata(p).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777
}

#[test]
fn install_twice_then_uninstall_is_identity() {
    for (target, rel, original) in [
        ("claude", ".claude/settings.json", FOREIGN_CLAUDE),
        ("codex", ".codex/hooks.json", r#"{"hooks": {"Stop": [{"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "notify"}]}]}}"#),
    ] {
        let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
        let path = home.path().join(rel);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&path, original).unwrap();
        assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), target, &[]).status.success());
        assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), target, &[]).status.success());
        let v = json_of(&path);
        assert_eq!(owned_count(&v), if target == "claude" { 6 } else { 5 }, "{target}: one entry per event");
        let out = uninstall(&dir, home.path(), target);
        assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
        assert_eq!(json_of(&path), serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(original).unwrap(), "{target}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn foreign_entries_keep_their_order_and_bytes() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&path, FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
    assert!(text.contains(r#"{"type": "command", "command": "say done"}"#), "{text}");
    let v = json_of(&path);
    assert_eq!(v["hooks"]["Stop"][0]["hooks"][0]["command"], "say done", "foreign group first");
    assert_eq!(v["hooks"]["PreToolUse"][0]["matcher"], "Bash");
    assert!(text.find("\"permissions\"").unwrap() < text.find("\"hooks\"").unwrap());
    assert!(text.find("\"hooks\"").unwrap() < text.find("\"model\"").unwrap());
}

#[test]
fn refuses_symlink_and_tracked_settings() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let claude = home.path().join(".claude");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&claude).unwrap();
    let real = home.path().join("dotfiles-settings.json");
    std::fs::write(&real, "{}").unwrap();
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&real, claude.join("settings.json")).unwrap();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("symlink"));
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&real).unwrap(), "{}", "untouched");

    std::fs::remove_file(claude.join("settings.json")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(claude.join("settings.json"), "{}").unwrap();
    let git = |args: &[&str]| std::process::Command::new("git").current_dir(&claude).args(args)
        .env("HOME", home.path()).env("GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1").env("GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null")
        .env("GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "t").env("GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "t@t").env("GIT_COMMITTER_NAME", "t").env("GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL", "t@t")
        .output().unwrap();
    git(&["init", "-q"]);
    git(&["add", "settings.json"]);
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    let err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
    assert!(err.contains("tracked by Git") && err.contains("--allow-tracked"), "{err}");
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &["--allow-tracked"]).status.success());
}

#[test]
fn uninstall_survives_claude_reformatting() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&path, FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    // Claude rewrites the whole file (indent 2) when it persists a rule (gate 2e).
    let mut v = json_of(&path);
    v["permissions"]["allow"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push("Bash(touch /tmp/*)".into());
    std::fs::write(&path, serde_json::to_string_pretty(&v).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert!(uninstall(&dir, home.path(), "claude").status.success());
    let mut want: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    want["permissions"]["allow"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push("Bash(touch /tmp/*)".into());
    assert_eq!(json_of(&path), want);
}

fn no_file_before_install_means_no_file_after_uninstall() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "codex", &[]).status.success());
    let path = home.path().join(".codex/hooks.json");
    assert!(path.exists());
    assert!(uninstall(&dir, home.path(), "codex").status.success());
    assert!(!path.exists());
}

fn codex_commands_call_the_absolute_binary_with_the_config_dir_and_marker() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "codex", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success());

    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement install and uninstall**

In `hooks_install/mod.rs`, delete `#![allow(dead_code)]` and extend the
imports:

```rust
use std::process::Command;

use anyhow::bail;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use serde_json::value::RawValue;

use self::json::{Obj, raw, raw_text};

pub const RECORD_FILE: &str = "hooks-installed.json";
```

Then add:

```rust
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Default, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct FileRecord {
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub created_file: bool,
    pub created_hooks_key: bool,
    pub created_events: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Default, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Record {
    pub version: u32,
    pub claude: Option<FileRecord>,
    pub codex: Option<FileRecord>,
}

impl Record {
    fn load(dir: &Path) -> Result<Record> {
        let p = dir.join(RECORD_FILE);
        if !p.exists() {
            return Ok(Record { version: 1, ..Default::default() });
        }
        Ok(serde_json::from_str(&crate::secrets::read_secret(&p)?)?)
    }
    fn save(&self, dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        crate::secrets::write_secret(&dir.join(RECORD_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(self)?)
    }
    fn slot(&mut self, t: Target) -> &mut Option<FileRecord> {
        match t {
            Target::Claude => &mut self.claude,
            Target::Codex => &mut self.codex,
        }
    }
}

pub struct Report {
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub backup: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub removed_file: bool,
}

/// Removes every owned entry from every event. Mixed groups keep their
/// foreign entries; a group of only owned entries is dropped.
fn strip_owned(hooks: &mut Obj) -> Result<()> {
    for (_, groups_raw) in hooks.0.iter_mut() {
        let Ok(groups) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<Box<RawValue>>>(groups_raw.get()) else { continue };
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        let mut changed = false;
        for g in groups {
            let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(g.get())?;
            let Some(list) = v.get("hooks").and_then(Value::as_array) else {
                out.push(g);
                continue;
            };
            let owned = list.iter().filter(|e| entries::is_owned_entry(e)).count();
            if owned == 0 {
                out.push(g);
                continue;
            }
            changed = true;
            if owned < list.len() {
                let mut v = v.clone();
                v["hooks"] = Value::Array(list.iter().filter(|e| !entries::is_owned_entry(e)).cloned().collect());
                out.push(raw(&v));
            }
        }
        if changed {
            *groups_raw = raw_text(render_array(&out))?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn render_array(items: &[Box<RawValue>]) -> String {
    if items.is_empty() {
        return "[]".into();
    }
    format!("[\n{}\n]", items.iter().map(|i| i.get().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(",\n"))
}

fn groups_for(ctx: &Ctx, t: Target) -> Result<Vec<(String, Value)>> {
    Ok(match t {
        Target::Claude => {
            let token = crate::secrets::read_secret(&ctx.config_dir.join("hook.token"))
                .context("hook.token is unreadable; run `acd init`")?;
            entries::claude_events(ctx.permission_hold)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|(e, m, t)| (e.to_string(), entries::claude_group(ctx, &token, e, m, t)))
                .collect()
        }
        Target::Codex => entries::CODEX_EVENTS.iter().map(|(e, t)| (e.to_string(), entries::codex_group(ctx, e, *t))).collect(),
    })
}

/// Refuses symlinks and foreign owners. `None` when the file is absent.
fn read_settings(path: &Path) -> Result<Option<String>> {
    use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;
    match std::fs::symlink_metadata(path) {
        Ok(m) if m.file_type().is_symlink() => bail!("refusing to edit {}: it is a symlink", path.display()),
        Ok(m) if m.uid() != acd_securefs::euid() => bail!("refusing to edit {}: it is not owned by you", path.display()),
        Ok(m) if !m.is_file() => bail!("refusing to edit {}: not a regular file", path.display()),
        Ok(_) => Ok(Some(std::fs::read_to_string(path)?)),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(e).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display())),
    }
}

/// Whether Git tracks `path`. Missing git means "cannot tell": allowed
/// with a warning.
fn git_tracked(path: &Path) -> bool {
    let (Some(dir), Some(name)) = (path.parent(), path.file_name()) else { return false };
    match Command::new("git")
        .arg("-C").arg(dir)
        .args(["ls-files", "--error-unmatch", "--"]).arg(name)
        .env("GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT", "0")
        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null()).stderr(std::process::Stdio::null())
        .status()
    {
        Ok(s) => s.success(),
        Err(_) => {
            eprintln!("warning: git not found; cannot check whether {} is tracked", path.display());
            false
        }
    }
}

fn backup(ctx: &Ctx, path: &Path, text: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    let dir = ctx.config_dir.join("backups");
    crate::secrets::ensure_private_dir(&dir)?;
    let ms = std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_millis();
    let name = path.file_name().map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    let b = dir.join(format!("{name}.{ms}.bak"));
    crate::secrets::write_atomic(&b, text.as_bytes(), 0o600)?;
    Ok(b)
}

fn file_mode(path: &Path, t: Target) -> u32 {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    match t {
        Target::Claude => 0o600, // holds the token (spec §6.1)
        Target::Codex => std::fs::metadata(path).map(|m| m.permissions().mode() & 0o777).unwrap_or(0o600),
    }
}

pub fn install(ctx: &Ctx, t: Target, allow_tracked: bool) -> Result<Report> {
    let path = ctx.settings_path(t);
    let existing = read_settings(&path)?;
    if t == Target::Claude && existing.is_some() && !allow_tracked && git_tracked(&path) {
        bail!(
            "{} is tracked by Git, and the hook token would be committed. Untrack it, or pass --allow-tracked",
            path.display()
        );
    }
    let mut record = Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    let mut rec = record.slot(t).clone().unwrap_or(FileRecord { path: path.clone(), created_file: existing.is_none(), ..Default::default() });
    let text = existing.clone().unwrap_or_default();
    let mut doc = Obj::parse(&text).with_context(|| format!("{} is not a JSON object", path.display()))?;
    let mut hooks = match doc.get("hooks") {
        Some(h) => Obj::parse(h.get()).with_context(|| format!("\"hooks\" in {} is not an object", path.display()))?,
        None => {
            rec.created_hooks_key = rec.created_hooks_key || doc.get("hooks").is_none();
            Obj::default()
        }
    };
    strip_owned(&mut hooks)?;
    for (event, group) in groups_for(ctx, t)? {
        let mut items: Vec<Box<RawValue>> = match hooks.get(&event) {
            Some(a) => serde_json::from_str(a.get()).with_context(|| format!("hooks.{event} is not an array"))?,
            None => {
                if !rec.created_events.contains(&event) {
                    rec.created_events.push(event.clone());
                }
                Vec::new()
            }
        };
        items.push(raw(&group));
        hooks.set(&event, raw_text(render_array(&items))?);
    }
    doc.set("hooks", raw_text(hooks.render())?);
    let backup = match &existing {
        Some(text) => Some(backup(ctx, &path, text)?),
        None => None,
    };
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
    }
    crate::secrets::write_atomic(&path, format!("{}\n", doc.render()).as_bytes(), file_mode(&path, t))?;
    *record.slot(t) = Some(rec);
    record.save(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    Ok(Report { path, backup, removed_file: false })
}

pub fn uninstall(ctx: &Ctx, t: Target) -> Result<Report> {
    let path = ctx.settings_path(t);
    let mut record = Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    let rec = record.slot(t).clone().unwrap_or(FileRecord { path: path.clone(), ..Default::default() });
    let Some(text) = read_settings(&path)? else {
        *record.slot(t) = None;
        record.save(&ctx.config_dir)?;
        return Ok(Report { path, backup: None, removed_file: false });
    };
    let mut doc = Obj::parse(&text)?;
    if let Some(h) = doc.get("hooks") {
        let mut hooks = Obj::parse(h.get())?;
        strip_owned(&mut hooks)?;
        for event in &rec.created_events {
            if hooks.get(event).is_some_and(|a| serde_json::from_str::<Vec<Value>>(a.get()).is_ok_and(|v| v.is_empty())) {
                hooks.remove(event);
            }
        }
        if hooks.is_empty() && rec.created_hooks_key {
            doc.remove("hooks");
        } else {
            doc.set("hooks", raw_text(hooks.render())?);
        }
    }
    let backup = Some(backup(ctx, &path, &text)?);
    let removed_file = rec.created_file && doc.is_empty();
    if removed_file {
        std::fs::remove_file(&path)?;
    } else {
        crate::secrets::write_atomic(&path, format!("{}\n", doc.render()).as_bytes(), file_mode(&path, t))?;
    }
    *record.slot(t) = None;
    record.save(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    Ok(Report { path, backup, removed_file })
}

/// Task 17: rewrites the token header of every owned Claude entry in the
/// recorded settings file. Returns the files updated.
pub fn rewrite_claude_token(ctx: &Ctx, token: &str) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {
    let record = Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    let Some(rec) = record.claude else { return Ok(vec![]) };
    let Some(text) = read_settings(&rec.path)? else { return Ok(vec![]) };
    let mut doc = Obj::parse(&text)?;
    let Some(h) = doc.get("hooks") else { return Ok(vec![]) };
    let mut hooks = Obj::parse(h.get())?;
    let mut changed = false;
    for (_, groups_raw) in hooks.0.iter_mut() {
        let Ok(mut groups) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<Value>>(groups_raw.get()) else { continue };
        let mut touched = false;
        for g in groups.iter_mut() {
            for e in g.get_mut("hooks").and_then(Value::as_array_mut).into_iter().flatten() {
                if entries::is_owned_entry(e) && e.get("headers").is_some() {
                    e["headers"]["X-Acd-Token"] = token.into();
                    touched = true;
                }
            }
        }
        if touched {
            *groups_raw = raw(&Value::Array(groups));
            changed = true;
        }
    }
    if !changed {
        return Ok(vec![]);
    }
    doc.set("hooks", raw_text(hooks.render())?);
    crate::secrets::write_atomic(&rec.path, format!("{}\n", doc.render()).as_bytes(), 0o600)
        .with_context(|| format!("updating {}", rec.path.display()))?;
    Ok(vec![rec.path])
}
```

### Task 17: Hook token rotation

PENDING GATE 7: spec §6.1 "Token storage" as amended by the gate 7
draft: running Claude sessions keep the token they started with, so the
previous token stays valid until no live session that started before
the rotation remains, at most 30 days. If the orchestrator keeps the
spec's 10-minute grace (open question Q4), `retire_if_done` becomes a
timestamp check (`now_ms - rotated_at_ms > 600_000`) and `pending` only
feeds the status message.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd/src/hook_token.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`token rotate hook`, preflight)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/mod.rs` (a successful Claude install completes a pending rotation)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/status.rs` (print source messages)
- Test: unit tests in `hook_token.rs`, `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 16b (`rewrite_claude_token`, `Ctx`), Task 14.
- Produces:
  - `hook_token::PREV_FILE = "hook.token.prev"`, `STATE_FILE = "hook-rotation.json"`, `MAX_RETENTION_DAYS = 30`.
  - `hook_token::RotationState { rotated_at_ms: i64, incomplete: bool, failed: Vec<String> }`.
  - `hook_token::HookTokens { current: String, previous: Option<String>, state: Option<RotationState> }`,
    `hook_token::load(dir: &Path) -> Result<HookTokens>`.
  - `hook_token::rotate(dir: &Path, ctx: &hooks_install::Ctx, now_ms: i64) -> Result<RotateReport>`
    with `RotateReport { updated: Vec<PathBuf>, failed: Vec<(PathBuf, String)>, dropped_pending: bool }`.
  - `hook_token::LiveSession { session_id: String, started_at_ms: Option<i64> }`.
  - `hook_token::pending(state: &RotationState, live: &[LiveSession], on_current: &HashSet<String>) -> usize`.
  - `hook_token::retire_if_done(dir: &Path, live: Option<&[LiveSession]>, on_current: &HashSet<String>, now_ms: i64) -> Result<Retire>`,
    `Retire { Nothing, Kept { pending: usize, incomplete: bool }, Retired }`.
  - `hook_token::mark_headers_updated(dir: &Path, now_ms: i64) -> Result<()>`.
  - `hook_token::status_message(state: Option<&RotationState>, pending: usize, rejected: usize) -> Option<(SourceState, String)>`.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn live(id: &str, started: Option<i64>) -> LiveSession {
        LiveSession { session_id: id.into(), started_at_ms: started }
    }
    fn state(at: i64, incomplete: bool) -> RotationState {
        RotationState { rotated_at_ms: at, incomplete, failed: vec![] }
    }

    #[test]
    fn pending_counts_sessions_started_before_the_rotation_and_not_seen_on_the_new_token() {
        let s = state(1_000, false);
        let on_new: HashSet<String> = ["b".to_string()].into();
        let sessions = [live("a", Some(500)), live("b", Some(600)), live("c", Some(2_000)), live("d", None)];
        assert_eq!(pending(&s, &sessions, &on_new), 2, "a (old) and d (unknown start); b moved, c is new");
    }

    #[test]
    fn retire_rules() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let d = dir.path();
        crate::secrets::write_secret(&d.join("hook.token"), &crate::secrets::new_token()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(retire_if_done(d, Some(&[]), &HashSet::new(), 0).unwrap(), Retire::Nothing);
        let write = |s: &RotationState| {
            crate::secrets::write_secret(&d.join(PREV_FILE), &crate::secrets::new_token()).unwrap();
            crate::secrets::write_secret(&d.join(STATE_FILE), &serde_json::to_string(s).unwrap()).unwrap();
        };
        write(&state(1_000, false));
        assert!(matches!(retire_if_done(d, None, &HashSet::new(), 2_000).unwrap(), Retire::Kept { .. }),
            "no complete scan yet: keep");
        assert!(matches!(retire_if_done(d, Some(&[live("a", Some(10))]), &HashSet::new(), 2_000).unwrap(), Retire::Kept { pending: 1, .. }));
        assert_eq!(retire_if_done(d, Some(&[]), &HashSet::new(), 2_000).unwrap(), Retire::Retired);
        assert!(!d.join(PREV_FILE).exists() && !d.join(STATE_FILE).exists());
        write(&state(1_000, true));
        assert!(matches!(retire_if_done(d, Some(&[]), &HashSet::new(), 2_000).unwrap(), Retire::Kept { incomplete: true, .. }),
            "an incomplete rotation keeps the old token");
        write(&state(1_000, false));
        let month = MAX_RETENTION_DAYS * 86_400_000 + 1_001;
        assert_eq!(retire_if_done(d, Some(&[live("a", Some(10))]), &HashSet::new(), month).unwrap(), Retire::Retired, "30-day cap");
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_messages() {
        assert_eq!(status_message(None, 0, 0), None);
        let (st, m) = status_message(Some(&state(0, true)), 0, 0).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st, SourceState::Degraded);
        assert!(m.contains("hook token rotation incomplete"), "{m}");
        let (st, m) = status_message(Some(&state(0, false)), 2, 0).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st, SourceState::Ok);
        assert!(m.contains("2 Claude sessions"), "{m}");
        let (st, m) = status_message(None, 0, 3).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st, SourceState::Degraded);
        assert!(m.contains("retired hook token"), "{m}");
    }
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn hook_rotation_rewrites_managed_headers_and_keeps_the_previous_token() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]).status.success());
    let old = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook.token")).unwrap().trim().to_string();
    let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).args(["token", "rotate", "hook"]).output().unwrap();
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    let new = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook.token")).unwrap().trim().to_string();
    assert_ne!(old, new);
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook.token.prev")).unwrap().trim(), old);
    let v = json_of(&home.path().join(".claude/settings.json"));
    assert_eq!(v["hooks"]["Stop"][0]["hooks"][0]["headers"]["X-Acd-Token"], new.as_str());
    let state: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook-rotation.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(state["incomplete"], false);
    for o in [&out.stdout, &out.stderr] {
        let t = String::from_utf8_lossy(o);
        assert!(!t.contains(&new) && !t.contains(&old), "hook tokens are never printed");
    }
    assert_eq!(mode(&dir.join("hook.token.prev")), 0o600);
    assert_eq!(mode(&dir.join("hook-rotation.json")), 0o600);
}

#[test]
fn a_failed_header_rewrite_fails_the_rotation_until_install_succeeds() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]).status.success());
    let settings = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    let moved = home.path().join("moved.json");
    std::fs::rename(&settings, &moved).unwrap();
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&moved, &settings).unwrap(); // the rewrite refuses symlinks
    let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).args(["token", "rotate", "hook"]).output().unwrap();
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("settings.json"), "names the file");
    let state: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook-rotation.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(state["incomplete"], true);
    std::fs::remove_file(&settings).unwrap();
    std::fs::rename(&moved, &settings).unwrap();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]).status.success());
    let state: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook-rotation.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(state["incomplete"], false);
}

#[test]
fn preflight_checks_the_rotation_files() {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    std::fs::write(dir.join("hook.token.prev"), "x").unwrap();
    std::fs::set_permissions(dir.join("hook.token.prev"), std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o644)).unwrap();
    let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).arg("status").output().unwrap();
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("hook.token.prev"));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `hook_token.rs`**

```rust
//! Hook token rotation (spec §6.1, gate 7 draft). Running Claude sessions
//! keep the token they started with, so the previous token stays valid
//! until no live registry session that started before the rotation
//! remains (sessions seen on the new token excluded), at most 30 days.
use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use acd_protocol::SourceState;
use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::secrets;

pub const PREV_FILE: &str = "hook.token.prev";
pub const STATE_FILE: &str = "hook-rotation.json";
pub const MAX_RETENTION_DAYS: i64 = 30;

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Default, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct RotationState {
    /// When the managed headers last carried the new token.
    pub rotated_at_ms: i64,
    pub incomplete: bool,
    pub failed: Vec<String>,
}

pub struct HookTokens {
    pub current: String,
    pub previous: Option<String>,
    pub state: Option<RotationState>,
}

pub fn load(dir: &Path) -> Result<HookTokens> {
    let current = secrets::read_secret(&dir.join("hook.token"))
        .and_then(|t| secrets::parse_token(&t))
        .context("hook.token is invalid; run `acd token rotate hook`")?;
    let previous = match dir.join(PREV_FILE).exists() {
        true => Some(secrets::read_secret(&dir.join(PREV_FILE))?).filter(|t| !t.is_empty()),
        false => None,
    };
    Ok(HookTokens { current, previous, state: read_state(dir)? })
}

fn read_state(dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<RotationState>> {
    let p = dir.join(STATE_FILE);
    if !p.exists() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    Ok(Some(serde_json::from_str(&secrets::read_secret(&p)?).context("hook-rotation.json is corrupt")?))
}

fn write_state(dir: &Path, s: &RotationState) -> Result<()> {
    secrets::write_secret(&dir.join(STATE_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(s)?)
}

pub struct RotateReport {
    pub updated: Vec<PathBuf>,
    pub failed: Vec<(PathBuf, String)>,
    /// A previous rotation was still pending; its token is now dropped (D12).
    pub dropped_pending: bool,
}

pub fn rotate(dir: &Path, ctx: &crate::hooks_install::Ctx, now_ms: i64) -> Result<RotateReport> {
    let old = secrets::read_secret(&dir.join("hook.token"))?;
    let dropped_pending = dir.join(PREV_FILE).exists();
    secrets::write_secret(&dir.join(PREV_FILE), &old)?;
    let new = secrets::new_token();
    secrets::write_secret(&dir.join("hook.token"), &new)?;
    let (updated, failed) = match crate::hooks_install::rewrite_claude_token(ctx, &new) {
        Ok(u) => (u, vec![]),
        Err(e) => (vec![], vec![(ctx.settings_path(crate::hooks_install::Target::Claude), format!("{e:#}"))]),
    };
    write_state(dir, &RotationState {
        rotated_at_ms: now_ms,
        incomplete: !failed.is_empty(),
        failed: failed.iter().map(|(p, _)| p.display().to_string()).collect(),
    })?;
    Ok(RotateReport { updated, failed, dropped_pending })
}

/// After a successful `acd hooks install claude`: the headers carry the
/// current token from now on.
pub fn mark_headers_updated(dir: &Path, now_ms: i64) -> Result<()> {
    if let Some(mut s) = read_state(dir)?
        && s.incomplete
    {
        s.incomplete = false;
        s.failed.clear();
        s.rotated_at_ms = now_ms;
        write_state(dir, &s)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct LiveSession {
    pub session_id: String,
    pub started_at_ms: Option<i64>,
}

pub fn pending(state: &RotationState, live: &[LiveSession], on_current: &HashSet<String>) -> usize {
    live.iter()
        .filter(|s| !on_current.contains(&s.session_id))
        .filter(|s| s.started_at_ms.is_none_or(|t| t < state.rotated_at_ms))
        .count()
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Retire {
    Nothing,
    Kept { pending: usize, incomplete: bool },
    Retired,
}

/// `live`: the last complete registry scan, `None` before one.
pub fn retire_if_done(dir: &Path, live: Option<&[LiveSession]>, on_current: &HashSet<String>, now_ms: i64) -> Result<Retire> {
    let Some(state) = read_state(dir)? else {
        return Ok(if dir.join(PREV_FILE).exists() { Retire::Kept { pending: 0, incomplete: false } } else { Retire::Nothing });
    };
    let n = live.map(|l| pending(&state, l, on_current));
    let expired = now_ms - state.rotated_at_ms > MAX_RETENTION_DAYS * 86_400_000;
    let done = !state.incomplete && (expired || n == Some(0));
    if !done {
        return Ok(Retire::Kept { pending: n.unwrap_or(0), incomplete: state.incomplete });
    }
    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(dir.join(PREV_FILE));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(dir.join(STATE_FILE));
    Ok(Retire::Retired)
}

pub fn status_message(state: Option<&RotationState>, pending: usize, rejected: usize) -> Option<(SourceState, String)> {
    if state.is_some_and(|s| s.incomplete) {
        return Some((SourceState::Degraded, "hook token rotation incomplete: run `acd hooks install claude`".into()));
    }
    if rejected > 0 {
        return Some((SourceState::Degraded, format!(
            "{rejected} Claude session(s) use a retired hook token; restart them or run /clear in them"
        )));
    }
    if pending > 0 {
        return Some((SourceState::Ok, format!(
            "hook token rotation pending: {pending} Claude sessions still use the previous token"
        )));
    }
    None
}
```

```rust
                    for (p, why) in &r.failed {
                        eprintln!("could not update {}: {why}", p.display());
                    }
                    bail!("hook token rotation incomplete; fix the file and run `acd hooks install claude`");
                }
                println!("Hook token rotated. The previous token stays valid until every Claude session started before now has exited (at most {} days).", hook_token::MAX_RETENTION_DAYS);
                Ok(())
            }
```

```rust
#[test]
fn sigterm_exits_cleanly_and_removes_the_socket() {
    let (_home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[]);
    let pid = started.daemon.0.id().to_string();
    assert!(std::process::Command::new("kill").args(["-TERM", &pid]).status().unwrap().success());
    let mut daemon = started.daemon;
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    let status = loop {
        if let Some(s) = daemon.0.try_wait().unwrap() { break s; }
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "acd did not exit on SIGTERM");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
    };
    assert_eq!(status.code(), Some(0), "launchd and systemd must not restart a clean stop");
    assert!(!dir.join("acd.sock").exists());
}

fn a_panic_exits_with_70_so_the_supervisor_restarts_acd() {
    let (_home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[("ACD_TEST_PANIC_AFTER_MS", "200")]);
    let mut daemon = started.daemon;
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    let status = loop {
        if let Some(s) = daemon.0.try_wait().unwrap() { break s; }
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "acd did not exit after a panic");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
    };
    assert_eq!(status.code(), Some(70));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `service.rs`**

```rust
//! `acd service install` (spec §9): a launchd agent on macOS, a systemd
//! user unit on Linux. Restarts on any non-zero exit (1 dead server, 70
//! panic, 75 Tailscale address change) and not after a clean stop (D15).
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::{Context, Result, bail};

pub const LABEL: &str = "dev.agent-caller.acd";
const PATH_MAC: &str = "/opt/homebrew/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin";
const PATH_LINUX: &str = "/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin";

pub trait Runner {
    fn run(&self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<()>;
}

pub struct SystemRunner;

impl Runner for SystemRunner {
    fn run(&self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
        let st = std::process::Command::new(program).args(args).status().with_context(|| format!("running {program}"))?;
        if !st.success() {
            bail!("{program} {} failed ({st})", args.join(" "));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Records calls instead of running them (debug builds, tests only).
struct FileRunner(PathBuf);

impl Runner for FileRunner {
    fn run(&self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
        use std::io::Write;
        let mut f = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(&self.0)?;
        writeln!(f, "{program} {}", args.join(" "))?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub fn runner() -> Box<dyn Runner> {
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if let Some(p) = std::env::var_os("ACD_TEST_SERVICE_RUNNER") {
        return Box::new(FileRunner(PathBuf::from(p)));
    }
    Box::new(SystemRunner)
}

fn xml(s: &str) -> String {
    s.replace('&', "&amp;").replace('<', "&lt;").replace('>', "&gt;")
}

pub fn launchd_plist(exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path, log: &Path) -> String {
    let (exe, dir, log) = (xml(&exe.to_string_lossy()), xml(&config_dir.to_string_lossy()), xml(&log.to_string_lossy()));
    format!(
r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
	<key>Label</key>
	<string>{LABEL}</string>
	<key>ProgramArguments</key>
	<array>
		<string>{exe}</string>
		<string>run</string>
	</array>
	<key>EnvironmentVariables</key>
	<dict>
		<key>ACD_CONFIG_DIR</key>
		<string>{dir}</string>
		<key>PATH</key>
		<string>{PATH_MAC}</string>
	</dict>
	<key>RunAtLoad</key>
	<true/>
	<key>KeepAlive</key>
	<dict>
		<key>SuccessfulExit</key>
		<false/>
	</dict>
	<key>ThrottleInterval</key>
	<integer>10</integer>
	<key>StandardErrorPath</key>
	<string>{log}</string>
	<key>StandardOutPath</key>
	<string>{log}</string>
</dict>
</plist>
"#
    )
}

fn unit_quote(s: &str) -> String {
    format!("\"{}\"", s.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('"', "\\\""))
}

pub fn systemd_unit(exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path) -> String {
    format!(
        "[Unit]\nDescription=agent-caller daemon (acd)\nAfter=network-online.target\n\n[Service]\nExecStart={} run\nEnvironment={}\nEnvironment=\"PATH={PATH_LINUX}\"\nRestart=on-failure\nRestartSec=2\n\n[Install]\nWantedBy=default.target\n",
        unit_quote(&exe.to_string_lossy()),
        unit_quote(&format!("ACD_CONFIG_DIR={}", config_dir.display())),
    )
}

pub fn install(home: &Path, exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path, print: bool, runner: &dyn Runner) -> Result<String> {
    if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
        let plist = home.join("Library/LaunchAgents").join(format!("{LABEL}.plist"));
        let text = launchd_plist(exe, config_dir, &home.join("Library/Logs/acd.log"));
        if print {
            return Ok(text);
        }
        std::fs::create_dir_all(plist.parent().expect("has parent"))?;
        std::fs::create_dir_all(home.join("Library/Logs"))?;
        crate::secrets::write_atomic(&plist, text.as_bytes(), 0o644)?;
        let uid = acd_securefs::euid();
        // Not loaded yet is fine.
        let _ = runner.run("launchctl", &["bootout".into(), format!("gui/{uid}/{LABEL}")]);
        runner.run("launchctl", &["bootstrap".into(), format!("gui/{uid}"), plist.display().to_string()])?;
        Ok(format!(
            "Installed and started {}. Stop it with: launchctl bootout gui/{uid}/{LABEL}",
            plist.display()
        ))
    } else if cfg!(target_os = "linux") {
        let unit = home.join(".config/systemd/user/acd.service");
        let text = systemd_unit(exe, config_dir);
        if print {
            return Ok(text);
        }
        std::fs::create_dir_all(unit.parent().expect("has parent"))?;
        crate::secrets::write_atomic(&unit, text.as_bytes(), 0o644)?;
        runner.run("systemctl", &["--user".into(), "daemon-reload".into()])?;
        runner.run("systemctl", &["--user".into(), "enable".into(), "--now".into(), "acd.service".into()])?;
        Ok(format!("Installed and started {}. Stop it with: systemctl --user disable --now acd.service", unit.display()))
    } else {
        bail!("acd service install supports macOS and Linux")
    }
}
```

`main.rs`:

```rust
mod service;

    /// Run acd under launchd (macOS) or a systemd user unit (Linux).
    Service {
        #[command(subcommand)]
        cmd: ServiceCmd,
    },

#[derive(Subcommand)]
enum ServiceCmd {
    Install {
        /// Print the service definition instead of installing it.
        #[arg(long)]
        print: bool,
    },
}

        Cmd::Service { cmd: ServiceCmd::Install { print } } => {
            if !dir.is_dir() {
                bail!("{} not initialized; run `acd init`", dir.display());
            }
            preflight(&dir)?;
            let home = std::path::PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os("HOME").context("HOME is not set")?);
            let exe = std::fs::canonicalize(std::env::current_exe()?)?;
            let dir = std::fs::canonicalize(&dir)?;
            println!("{}", service::install(&home, &exe, &dir, print, service::runner().as_ref())?);
            Ok(())
        }
```

`daemon.rs`:

```rust
/// A server task stopped: the supervisor restarts acd.
pub const EXIT_SERVER_STOPPED: i32 = 1;
/// Any panic, including a poisoned mutex (D14).
pub const EXIT_PANIC: i32 = 70;

fn exit_on_panic() {
    let default = std::panic::take_hook();
    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |info| {
        default(info);
        eprintln!("acd: panic; exiting so the supervisor restarts acd");
        std::process::exit(EXIT_PANIC);
    }));
}
```

At the top of `run`: `exit_on_panic();`, then in debug builds:

```rust
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if let Some(ms) = std::env::var("ACD_TEST_PANIC_AFTER_MS").ok().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()) {
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(ms)).await;
            panic!("test panic");
        });
    }
```

Collect every server into one `tokio::task::JoinSet<String>` instead of
detached `tokio::spawn` calls. Each TCP listener task returns
`format!("listener {addr}")`, the Unix socket task `"local socket"`:

```rust
    let mut servers = tokio::task::JoinSet::new();
    // for each TCP listener:
    servers.spawn(async move {
        if let Err(e) = axum::serve(tls_listener, app.into_make_service_with_connect_info::<core_tls::Admit>()).await {
            tracing::error!(%addr, error = %e, "server stopped");
        }
        format!("listener {addr}")
    });
```

Replace the final `select!` with:

```rust
    let mut term = tokio::signal::unix::signal(tokio::signal::unix::SignalKind::terminate())?;
    let exit_code = tokio::select! {
        _ = tokio::signal::ctrl_c() => None,
        _ = term.recv() => None,
        _ = watch => Some(EXIT_ADDRESSES_CHANGED),
        Some(done) = servers.join_next() => {
            tracing::error!(server = %done.unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string()), "a server task stopped; exiting");
            Some(EXIT_SERVER_STOPPED)
        }
    };
```

```rust
pub fn post_hook(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> (u16, String) {
    use std::io::{Read, Write};
    let mut s = std::net::TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).unwrap();
    s.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5))).unwrap();
    let mut req = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\nX-Acd-Token: {token}\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    for (k, v) in headers {
        req.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\r\n"));
    }
    req.push_str("\r\n");
    req.push_str(body);
    s.write_all(req.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let mut out = String::new();
    s.read_to_string(&mut out).unwrap();
    let status = out.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|c| c.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    (status, out.split_once("\r\n\r\n").map(|(_, b)| b.to_owned()).unwrap_or_default())
}
```

```rust
fn a_taken_hook_port_is_fatal() {

    let hook_port = blocker.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    std::fs::write(dir.join("config.toml"), format!("port = {}\nhook_port = {hook_port}\nlisten = [\"loopback\"]\n", free_port())).unwrap();

    assert!(!status.success());

}
```

After the acd/1 listeners are bound and before serving (D13):

```rust
    let hook_auth = Arc::new(acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::HookAuth::new(hook_tokens.current.clone()));
    hook_auth.set(hook_tokens.current.clone(), hook_tokens.previous.clone());
    let hook_addr = SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], cfg.hook_port));
    let hook_listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(hook_addr)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("cannot bind hook endpoint {hook_addr}: {e}"))?;
    tracing::info!(addr = %hook_addr, "hook endpoint listening");
    let hook_app = hooks.router(hook_auth.clone());
    servers.spawn(async move {
        if let Err(e) = axum::serve(hook_listener, hook_app).await {
            tracing::error!(error = %e, "hook endpoint stopped");
        }
        "hook endpoint".to_string()
    });
    core.set_source_status(Source::Hooks, SourceStatus { state: SourceState::Ok, message: None });
```

(If Task 7 had to switch to its own `endpoint::serve`, call that here
instead of `axum::serve`.)

The hook token refresher, next to the client token refresher:

```rust
    let hook_dir = dir.to_path_buf();
    let (refresh_auth, refresh_hooks, refresh_core) = (hook_auth.clone(), hooks.clone(), core.clone());
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut last_retire = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        let started = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        loop {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
            let Ok(t) = crate::hook_token::load(&hook_dir) else {
                tracing::error!("hook token unreadable or invalid; keeping the current tokens");
                continue;
            };
            refresh_auth.set(t.current.clone(), t.previous.clone());
            if last_retire.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(30) {
                last_retire = tokio::time::Instant::now();
                let live: Option<Vec<crate::hook_token::LiveSession>> = refresh_hooks.live_sessions().map(|l| {
                    l.into_iter()
                        .map(|e| crate::hook_token::LiveSession { session_id: e.session_id, started_at_ms: e.started_at_ms })
                        .collect()
                });
                let now = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
                let on_current = refresh_hooks.current_token_sessions();
                let pending = match crate::hook_token::retire_if_done(&hook_dir, live.as_deref(), &on_current, now) {
                    Ok(crate::hook_token::Retire::Kept { pending, .. }) => pending,
                    Ok(_) => 0,
                    Err(e) => {
                        tracing::warn!(error = %e, "could not evaluate the hook token rotation");
                        0
                    }
                };
                let rejected = refresh_auth.rejected_since(tokio::time::Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(600).min(started.elapsed()));
                let state = crate::hook_token::load(&hook_dir).ok().and_then(|t| t.state);
                let status = match crate::hook_token::status_message(state.as_ref(), pending, rejected) {
                    Some((st, m)) => SourceStatus { state: st, message: Some(m) },
                    None => SourceStatus { state: SourceState::Ok, message: None },
                };
                refresh_core.set_source_status(Source::Hooks, status);
            }
        }
    });
```

Imports: `acd_protocol::{Machine, Source, SourceState, SourceStatus, Sources}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the end-to-end tests**

`crates/acd-adapters/tests/hooks_e2e.rs` drives the real endpoint, the
herdr adapter over FakeHerdr, and the core, and measures the §14 bounds:

```rust
mod common;
use common::*;

use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::fake::fake_agent;
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::read_response;
use acd_protocol::*;
use tokio::time::Instant;

fn herdr_screen(name: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

async fn answer(r: &Rig, id: &str, rid: &str, option: &str) -> CommandResult {
    r.core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
        id: id.into(), request_id: rid.into(), revision: None, option_id: Some(option.into()), text: None,
    }).await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn criterion_1_a_herdr_bound_claude_permission_is_answerable_within_2_s_and_yes_unblocks() {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")], |_| {}).await;
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("claude-permission-bash.txt"));
    let agent = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().keys().next().unwrap().clone();
    let sock = r.sock();
    let h = [("X-Herdr-Pane", "w1:p1"), ("X-Herdr-Socket", sock.as_str()), ("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")];
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let t = Instant::now();
    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestOpened { request, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request.kind == RequestKind::Permission
        {
            break request.id;
        }
    };
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(2), "{:?}", t.elapsed());
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(read_response(held).await.1, acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_allow());
    assert_eq!(answer(&r, "a2", &rid, "deny").await.error_code(), Some(ErrorCode::RequestBusy),
        "the agent applies only the first answer");
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().agent_id, agent);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn criterion_4_answered_in_the_terminal_first_gives_request_gone() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &CLI).await;
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    drop(held); // terminal deny
    let mut stop = fx("claude/payload-Stop-after-deny.json");
    stop["session_id"] = SID.into();
    r.hook("claude", "Stop", &stop, &CLI).await;
    until("resolved", || r.core.request(&rid).is_none().then_some(())).await;
    assert_eq!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await.error_code(), Some(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
}
```

`lifecycle_hooks_reply_at_once_without_a_consumer`; the install round
trip by Task 16b.)

1. A Claude permission in a herdr pane appears within 2 s; "Yes" from

2. Terminal "Yes" while held: the card resolves `terminal` within about

4. A single-select `AskUserQuestion` answered from the inbox.
5. A Codex approval in an embedded-mode Codex pane (one Codex pane in

8. `acd token rotate hook` while a Claude session runs: the session's

9. `acd service install` on macOS: the agent starts, `kill -TERM` stops
